\displaystyle\cdot\mathbb{E}\left(\ddot{e}_{t_{1},k,j_{1}}\ddot{e}_{s_{1},k,j_{1}}\ddot{e}_{t_{2},k,j_{2}}\ddot{e}_{s_{2},k,j_{2}}\right).

We deal with the terms separately. For the term related to
\ddot{f}
, we separate the cases for
i_{1}=i_{2}
and
i_{1}\neq i_{2}
. When
i_{1}=i_{2}
, by Assumption (F1), we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left(\ddot{f}_{t_{1},k,i_{1}}\ddot{f}_{s_{1},k,i_{1}}\ddot{f}_{t_{2},k,i_{2}}\ddot{f}_{s_{2},k,i_{2}}\right)=\mathbb{E}\left(\ddot{f}_{t_{1},k,i_{1}}\ddot{f}_{s_{1},k,i_{1}}\ddot{f}_{t_{2},k,i_{1}}\ddot{f}_{s_{2},k,i_{1}}\right)=\left(\mathbf{A}_{f,T^{2}}\right)_{t_{1}s_{1},t_{2}s_{2}},

where
\left(\mathbf{A}_{f,T^{2}}\right)_{t_{1}s_{1},t_{2}s_{2}}
is the
(t_{1}s_{1},t_{2}s_{2})
entry of
\mathbf{A}_{f,T^{2}}
. When
i_{1}\neq i_{2}
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left(\ddot{f}_{t_{1},k,i_{1}}\ddot{f}_{s_{1},k,i_{1}}\ddot{f}_{t_{2},k,i_{2}}\ddot{f}_{s_{2},k,i_{2}}\right)=\mathbb{E}\left(\ddot{f}_{t_{1},k,i_{1}}\ddot{f}_{s_{1},k,i_{1}}\right)\mathbb{E}\left(\ddot{f}_{t_{2},k,i_{2}}\ddot{f}_{s_{2},k,i_{2}}\right)=\left(\mathbf{A}_{f,T}\right)_{t_{1}s_{1}}\left(\mathbf{A}_{f,T}\right)_{t_{2}s_{2}}.

Next, for the term related to
\ddot{e}
, by Assumption (E1) and (E2),

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left(\ddot{e}_{t_{1},k,j_{1}}\ddot{e}_{s_{1},k,j_{1}}\ddot{e}_{t_{2},k,j_{2}}\ddot{e}_{s_{2},k,j_{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{s_{\text{-}k}^{2}}\left[\left(\boldsymbol{\Psi}^{(k)}\boldsymbol{\Psi}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}\right)_{j_{1}j_{1}}\left(\boldsymbol{\Psi}^{(k)}\boldsymbol{\Psi}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}\right)_{j_{2}j_{2}}\left(\mathbf{A}_{e,T^{2}}\right)_{t_{1}s_{1},t_{2}s_{2}}+\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\epsilon,l}^{(k)})_{j_{1}j_{1}}(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\epsilon,l}^{(k)})_{j_{2}j_{2}}\left(\mathbf{A}_{\epsilon,T^{2}}\right)_{t_{1}s_{1},t_{2}s_{2}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{s_{\text{-}k}^{2}}\left[\left(\boldsymbol{\Psi}^{(k)}\boldsymbol{\Psi}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}\right)_{j_{1}j_{1}}\left(\boldsymbol{\Psi}^{(k)}\boldsymbol{\Psi}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}\right)_{j_{2}j_{2}}\left(\mathbf{A}_{e,T^{2}}\right)_{t_{1}s_{1},t_{2}s_{2}}+(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\epsilon}^{(k)})_{j_{1}j_{1}}(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\epsilon}^{(k)})_{j_{2}j_{2}}\left(\mathbf{A}_{\epsilon,T^{2}}\right)_{t_{1}s_{1},t_{2}s_{2}}\right].

(8.33)

Hence, when
i_{1}=i_{2}
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[(\ddot{\mathbf{F}}_{k}\ddot{\mathbf{E}}_{k})_{i_{1}j_{1}}^{2}(\ddot{\mathbf{F}}_{k}\ddot{\mathbf{E}}_{k})_{i_{2}j_{2}}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{s_{\text{-}k}^{2}}\bigg[\left(\boldsymbol{\Psi}^{(k)}\boldsymbol{\Psi}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}\right)_{j_{1}j_{1}}\left(\boldsymbol{\Psi}^{(k)}\boldsymbol{\Psi}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}\right)_{j_{2}j_{2}}\text{vec}{(\mathbf{A}_{f,T^{2}})}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\text{vec}{(\mathbf{A}_{e,T^{2}})}

\displaystyle+(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\epsilon}^{(k)})_{j_{1}j_{1}}(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\epsilon}^{(k)})_{j_{2}j_{2}}\text{vec}{(\mathbf{A}_{f,T^{2}})}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\text{vec}{(\mathbf{A}_{\epsilon,T^{2}})}\bigg],

and when
i_{1}\neq i_{2}
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[(\ddot{\mathbf{F}}_{k}\ddot{\mathbf{E}}_{k})_{i_{1}j_{1}}^{2}(\ddot{\mathbf{F}}_{k}\ddot{\mathbf{E}}_{k})_{i_{2}j_{2}}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{s_{\text{-}k}^{2}}\bigg[\left(\boldsymbol{\Psi}^{(k)}\boldsymbol{\Psi}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}\right)_{j_{1}j_{1}}\left(\boldsymbol{\Psi}^{(k)}\boldsymbol{\Psi}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}\right)_{j_{2}j_{2}}\text{vec}{(\mathbf{A}_{f,T}\otimes\mathbf{A}_{f,T})}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\text{vec}{(\mathbf{A}_{e,T^{2}})}

\displaystyle+(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\epsilon}^{(k)})_{j_{1}j_{1}}(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\epsilon}^{(k)})_{j_{2}j_{2}}\text{vec}{(\mathbf{A}_{f,T}\otimes\mathbf{A}_{f,T})}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\text{vec}{(\mathbf{A}_{\epsilon,T^{2}})}\bigg].

Thus, by Lemma
3
, we have for any
(i_{1},j_{1},i_{2},j_{2})
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[(\ddot{\mathbf{F}}_{k}\ddot{\mathbf{E}}_{k})_{i_{1}j_{1}}^{2}(\ddot{\mathbf{F}}_{k}\ddot{\mathbf{E}}_{k})_{i_{2}j_{2}}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle O\left(T^{2}\frac{1}{s_{\text{-}k}^{2}}\right)\left[\left(\boldsymbol{\Psi}^{(k)}\boldsymbol{\Psi}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}\right)_{j_{1}j_{1}}\left(\boldsymbol{\Psi}^{(k)}\boldsymbol{\Psi}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}\right)_{j_{2}j_{2}}+(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\epsilon}^{(k)})_{j_{1}j_{1}}(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\epsilon}^{(k)})_{j_{2}j_{2}}\right].

Therefore,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left\|\ddot{\mathbf{F}}_{k}\ddot{\mathbf{E}}_{k}\right\|_{F}^{4}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{r_{k}}\sum_{j_{1}=1}^{d_{k}}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{r_{k}}\sum_{j_{2}=1}^{d_{k}}\mathbb{E}\left[(\ddot{\mathbf{F}}_{k}\ddot{\mathbf{E}}_{k})_{i_{1}j_{1}}^{2}(\ddot{\mathbf{F}}_{k}\ddot{\mathbf{E}}_{k})_{i_{2}j_{2}}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq r_{k}^{2}O\left(T^{2}\frac{1}{s_{\text{-}k}^{2}}\right)\left\{\left[\text{tr}{(\boldsymbol{\Psi}^{(k)}\boldsymbol{\Psi}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}})}\right]^{2}+\left[\text{tr}{(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\epsilon}^{(k)})}\right]^{2}\right\}=O\left(r_{k}^{2}T^{2}d_{k}^{2}\frac{d_{\text{-}k}^{2}}{s_{\text{-}k}^{2}}\right),

(8.34)

by Assumption (E1). (
8
) together with (
8
) and (
8
) imply
\left\|\ddot{\mathbf{F}}_{k}\ddot{\mathbf{E}}_{k}\right\|_{F}^{2}=O_{p}\left(r_{k}d_{k}T\frac{d_{\text{-}k}}{s_{\text{-}k}}\right)
.

Next, we will show
\left\|\ddot{\mathbf{F}}_{k}\frac{1}{T}\mathbf{1}_{T}\mathbf{1}_{T}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\ddot{\mathbf{E}}_{k}\right\|_{F}^{2}=O_{p}\left(r_{k}d_{k}\frac{d_{\text{-}k}}{s_{\text{-}k}}\right)
in a similar manner. First, note that

\displaystyle\left(\ddot{\mathbf{F}}_{k}\frac{1}{T}\mathbf{1}_{T}\mathbf{1}_{T}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\ddot{\mathbf{E}}_{k}\right)_{ij}=\frac{1}{T}\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\ddot{f}_{t,k,i}\right)\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\ddot{e}_{t,k,j}\right),

and

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left(\ddot{\mathbf{F}}_{k}\frac{1}{T}\mathbf{1}_{T}\mathbf{1}_{T}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\ddot{\mathbf{E}}_{k}\right)_{ij}^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T^{2}}\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T}\sum_{s_{1}=1}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=1}^{T}\sum_{s_{2}=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left(\ddot{f}_{t_{1},k,i}\ddot{f}_{s_{1},k,i}\right)\mathbb{E}\left(\ddot{e}_{t_{2},k,j}\ddot{e}_{s_{2},k,j}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T^{2}}\sum_{t_{1},t_{2},s_{1},s_{2}=1}^{T}(\mathbf{A}_{f,T})_{t_{1}s_{1}}\frac{1}{s_{\text{-}k}}\left[\left(\boldsymbol{\Psi}^{(k)}\boldsymbol{\Psi}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}\right)_{jj}\left(\mathbf{A}_{e,T}\right)_{t_{2}s_{2}}+\left(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\epsilon}^{(k)}\right)_{jj}\left(\mathbf{A}_{\epsilon,T}\right)_{t_{2}s_{2}}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T^{2}s_{\text{-}k}}\left[\left(\boldsymbol{\Psi}^{(k)}\boldsymbol{\Psi}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}\right)_{jj}\mathbf{1}_{T^{2}}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{A}_{f,T}\otimes\mathbf{A}_{e,T}\mathbf{1}_{T^{2}}+\left(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\epsilon}^{(k)}\right)_{jj}\mathbf{1}_{T^{2}}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{A}_{f,T}\otimes\mathbf{A}_{\epsilon,T}\mathbf{1}_{T^{2}}\right]

\displaystyle=O\left(\frac{1}{s_{\text{-}k}}\right)\left[\left(\boldsymbol{\Psi}^{(k)}\boldsymbol{\Psi}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}\right)_{jj}+\left(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\epsilon}^{(k)}\right)_{jj}\right]

where the second line follows from (
8.30
) and (
8.31
), and the last line follows from Lemma
3
. Hence,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left\|\ddot{\mathbf{F}}_{k}\frac{1}{T}\mathbf{1}_{T}\mathbf{1}_{T}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\ddot{\mathbf{E}}_{k}\right\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{r_{k}}\sum_{j=1}^{d_{k}}O\left(\frac{1}{s_{\text{-}k}}\right)\left[\left(\boldsymbol{\Psi}^{(k)}\boldsymbol{\Psi}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}\right)_{jj}+\left(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\epsilon}^{(k)}\right)_{jj}\right]

\displaystyle=O\left(r_{k}\frac{1}{s_{\text{-}k}}\right)\left[\text{tr}{(\boldsymbol{\Psi}^{(k)}\boldsymbol{\Psi}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}})}+\text{tr}{(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\epsilon}^{(k)})}\right]=O\left(r_{k}d_{k}\frac{d_{\text{-}k}}{s_{\text{-}k}}\right).

(8.35)

Next, we know

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left\|\ddot{\mathbf{F}}_{k}\frac{1}{T}\mathbf{1}_{T}\mathbf{1}_{T}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\ddot{\mathbf{E}}_{k}\right\|_{F}^{4}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T^{4}}\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{r_{k}}\sum_{j_{1}=1}^{d_{k}}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{r_{k}}\sum_{j_{2}=1}^{d_{k}}\mathbb{E}\left[(\ddot{\mathbf{F}}_{k}\mathbf{1}_{T}\mathbf{1}_{T}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\ddot{\mathbf{E}}_{k})_{i_{1}j_{1}}^{2}(\ddot{\mathbf{F}}_{k}\mathbf{1}_{T}\mathbf{1}_{T}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\ddot{\mathbf{E}}_{k})_{i_{2}j_{2}}^{2}\right],

where

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[(\ddot{\mathbf{F}}_{k}\mathbf{1}_{T}\mathbf{1}_{T}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\ddot{\mathbf{E}}_{k})_{i_{1}j_{1}}^{2}(\ddot{\mathbf{F}}_{k}\mathbf{1}_{T}\mathbf{1}_{T}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\ddot{\mathbf{E}}_{k})_{i_{2}j_{2}}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\ddot{f}_{t,k,i_{1}}\right)^{2}\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\ddot{f}_{t,k,i_{2}}\right)^{2}\right]\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\ddot{e}_{t,k,j_{1}}\right)^{2}\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\ddot{e}_{t,k,j_{2}}\right)^{2}\right].

For the terms related to
\ddot{f}
, when
i_{1}=i_{2}
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\ddot{f}_{t,k,i_{1}}\right)^{2}\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\ddot{f}_{t,k,i_{2}}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\ddot{f}_{t,k,i_{1}}\right)^{4}\right]=\sum_{t_{1},t_{2},s_{1},s_{2}=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left[\ddot{f}_{t_{1},k,i_{1}}\ddot{f}_{s_{1},k,i_{1}}\ddot{f}_{t_{2},k,i_{1}}\ddot{f}_{s_{2},k,i_{1}}\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{t_{1},t_{2},s_{1},s_{2}=1}^{T}\left(\mathbf{A}_{f,T^{2}}\right)_{t_{1}s_{1},t_{2}s_{2}}=\mathbf{1}_{T^{2}}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{A}_{f,T^{2}}\mathbf{1}_{T^{2}}=O(T^{2})

by Lemma
3
. When
i_{1}\neq i_{2}
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\ddot{f}_{t,k,i_{1}}\right)^{2}\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\ddot{f}_{t,k,i_{2}}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\ddot{f}_{t,k,i_{1}}\right)^{2}\right]\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\ddot{f}_{t,k,i_{2}}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\left[\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{s=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left(\ddot{f}_{t,k,i_{1}}\ddot{f}_{s,k,i_{1}}\right)\right]^{2}=\left(\mathbf{1}_{T}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{A}_{f,T}\mathbf{1}_{T}\right)^{2}=O(T^{2})

by Lemma
3
. Therefore,
\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\ddot{f}_{t,k,i_{1}}\right)^{2}\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\ddot{f}_{t,k,i_{2}}\right)^{2}\right]=O(T^{2})
for any
(i_{1},i_{2})
. Finally, for terms with
\ddot{e}
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\ddot{e}_{t,k,j_{1}}\right)^{2}\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\ddot{e}_{t,k,j_{2}}\right)^{2}\right]=\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T}\sum_{s_{1}=1}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=1}^{T}\sum_{s_{2}=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left[\ddot{e}_{t_{1},k,j_{1}}\ddot{e}_{s_{1},k,j_{1}}\ddot{e}_{t_{2},k,j_{2}}\ddot{e}_{s_{2},k,j_{2}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{t_{1},t_{2},s_{1},s_{2}=1}^{T}\frac{1}{s_{\text{-}k}^{2}}\left[\left(\boldsymbol{\Psi}^{(k)}\boldsymbol{\Psi}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}\right)_{j_{1}j_{1}}\left(\boldsymbol{\Psi}^{(k)}\boldsymbol{\Psi}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}\right)_{j_{2}j_{2}}\left(\mathbf{A}_{e,T^{2}}\right)_{t_{1}s_{1},t_{2}s_{2}}+(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\epsilon}^{(k)})_{j_{1}j_{1}}(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\epsilon}^{(k)})_{j_{2}j_{2}}\left(\mathbf{A}_{\epsilon,T^{2}}\right)_{t_{1}s_{1},t_{2}s_{2}}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{s_{\text{-}k}^{2}}\left[\left(\boldsymbol{\Psi}^{(k)}\boldsymbol{\Psi}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}\right)_{j_{1}j_{1}}\left(\boldsymbol{\Psi}^{(k)}\boldsymbol{\Psi}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}\right)_{j_{2}j_{2}}\mathbf{1}_{T^{2}}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{A}_{e,T^{2}}\mathbf{1}_{T^{2}}+(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\epsilon}^{(k)})_{j_{1}j_{1}}(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\epsilon}^{(k)})_{j_{2}j_{2}}\mathbf{1}_{T^{2}}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{A}_{\epsilon,T^{2}}\mathbf{1}_{T^{2}}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O\left(\frac{T^{2}}{s_{\text{-}k}^{2}}\right)\left[\left(\boldsymbol{\Psi}^{(k)}\boldsymbol{\Psi}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}\right)_{j_{1}j_{1}}\left(\boldsymbol{\Psi}^{(k)}\boldsymbol{\Psi}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}\right)_{j_{2}j_{2}}+(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\epsilon}^{(k)})_{j_{1}j_{1}}(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\epsilon}^{(k)})_{j_{2}j_{2}}\right],

where the third line follows from (
8
), and the last line from Lemma
3
. Therefore,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[(\ddot{\mathbf{F}}_{k}\mathbf{1}_{T}\mathbf{1}_{T}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\ddot{\mathbf{E}}_{k})_{i_{1}j_{1}}^{2}(\ddot{\mathbf{F}}_{k}\mathbf{1}_{T}\mathbf{1}_{T}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\ddot{\mathbf{E}}_{k})_{i_{2}j_{2}}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=O\left(\frac{T^{4}}{s_{\text{-}k}^{2}}\right)\left[\left(\boldsymbol{\Psi}^{(k)}\boldsymbol{\Psi}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}\right)_{j_{1}j_{1}}\left(\boldsymbol{\Psi}^{(k)}\boldsymbol{\Psi}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}\right)_{j_{2}j_{2}}+(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\epsilon}^{(k)})_{j_{1}j_{1}}(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\epsilon}^{(k)})_{j_{2}j_{2}}\right].

Hence,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left\|\ddot{\mathbf{F}}_{k}\frac{1}{T}\mathbf{1}_{T}\mathbf{1}_{T}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\ddot{\mathbf{E}}_{k}\right\|_{F}^{4}

\displaystyle=O\left(\frac{1}{s_{\text{-}k}^{2}}\right)\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{r_{k}}\sum_{j_{1}=1}^{d_{k}}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{r_{k}}\sum_{j_{2}=1}^{d_{k}}\left[\left(\boldsymbol{\Psi}^{(k)}\boldsymbol{\Psi}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}\right)_{j_{1}j_{1}}\left(\boldsymbol{\Psi}^{(k)}\boldsymbol{\Psi}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}\right)_{j_{2}j_{2}}+(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\epsilon}^{(k)})_{j_{1}j_{1}}(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\epsilon}^{(k)})_{j_{2}j_{2}}\right]

\displaystyle=r_{k}^{2}O\left(\frac{1}{s_{\text{-}k}^{2}}\right)\left\{\left[\text{tr}{(\boldsymbol{\Psi}^{(k)}\boldsymbol{\Psi}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}})}\right]^{2}+\left[\text{tr}{(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\epsilon}^{(k)})}\right]^{2}\right\}=O\left(r_{k}^{2}d_{k}^{2}\frac{d_{\text{-}k}^{2}}{s_{\text{-}k}^{2}}\right).

(8.36)

(
8
) together with (
8
) imply
\left\|\ddot{\mathbf{F}}_{k}\frac{1}{T}\mathbf{1}_{T}\mathbf{1}_{T}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\ddot{\mathbf{E}}_{k}\right\|_{F}^{2}=O_{p}\left(r_{k}d_{k}\frac{d_{\text{-}k}}{s_{\text{-}k}}\right)
. Therefore, we can conclude that

\displaystyle\bigg\|\frac{\mathbf{A}_{k}\ddot{\mathbf{F}}_{k}\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}-\frac{1}{T}\mathbf{1}_{T}\mathbf{1}_{T}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\right)\ddot{\mathbf{E}}_{k}}{T}\bigg\|^{2}

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(d_{k}^{\alpha_{k,1}}\right)\left(\bigg\|\frac{\ddot{\mathbf{F}}_{k}\ddot{\mathbf{E}}_{k}}{T}\bigg\|^{2}+\bigg\|\frac{\ddot{\mathbf{F}}_{k}\frac{1}{T}\mathbf{1}_{T}\mathbf{1}_{T}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\ddot{\mathbf{E}}_{k}}{T}\bigg\|^{2}\right)=O_{p}\left(\frac{d_{\text{-}k}}{s_{\text{-}k}}\frac{r_{k}d_{k}}{T}d_{k}^{\alpha_{k,1}}\right).

(8.37)

Compared with the original rate (
8
), we can obtain a potentially better rate (
8.37
) by directly bounding and counting the elements in a large matrix. In other words, the rate for
\bigg\|\frac{\mathbf{A}_{k}\ddot{\mathbf{F}}_{k}\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}-\frac{1}{T}\mathbf{1}_{T}\mathbf{1}_{T}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\right)\ddot{\mathbf{E}}_{k}}{T}\bigg\|^{2}
will be the minimum between the two, which is

\displaystyle\bigg\|\frac{\mathbf{A}_{k}\ddot{\mathbf{F}}_{k}\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}-\frac{1}{T}\mathbf{1}_{T}\mathbf{1}_{T}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\right)\ddot{\mathbf{E}}_{k}}{T}\bigg\|^{2}

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\frac{d_{\text{-}k}}{s_{\text{-}k}}d_{k}^{\alpha_{k,1}}\min{\left\{1+\frac{d_{k}}{T},\frac{r_{k}d_{k}}{T}\right\}}\right).

Combining the rates (
8
), (
8.37
) and (
8.28
) yields that

\displaystyle\big\|R_{k}\big\|^{2}=\frac{d_{\text{-}k}^{2}}{s_{\text{-}k}^{2}}\left(1+\frac{d_{k}^{2}}{T^{2}}\right)+\frac{d_{\text{-}k}}{s_{\text{-}k}}d_{k}^{\alpha_{k,1}}\min{\left\{1+\frac{d_{k}}{T},\frac{r_{k}d_{k}}{T}\right\}}:=c_{k}.

Hence,

\displaystyle\big\|\widehat{\mathbf{U}}_{k,(z_{k})}-\mathbf{U}_{k,(z_{k})}\big\|^{2}=O_{p}\left(d_{k}^{-2\alpha_{k,z_{k}}}\left[d_{k}^{2\alpha_{k,1}}\frac{r_{k}}{T}+c_{k}\right]\right).

Finally, to show (
3.10
), let
\widetilde{\mathbf{V}}_{k}
be the
z_{k}\times z_{k}
diagonal matrix of the first
z_{k}
largest eigenvalues of
\widehat{\Sigma}_{\widetilde{\mathbf{x}}_{k}}
in decreasing order, and
\ddot{\mathbf{V}}_{k}:=\widetilde{\mathbf{V}}_{k}/s_{\text{-}k}
. Then it follows from (
8
) that

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{k,(z_{k})}-\mathbf{Q}_{k}\ddot{\mathbf{H}}_{k}

\displaystyle=R_{k}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{k,(z_{k})}\ddot{\mathbf{V}}_{k}^{-1},\text{ implying }

\displaystyle\big\|\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{k,(z_{k})}-\mathbf{Q}_{k}\ddot{\mathbf{H}}_{k}\big\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\big\|R_{k}\big\|^{2}\big\|\ddot{\mathbf{V}}_{k}^{-1}\big\|^{2}O_{p}\left(d_{k}^{-2\alpha_{k,z_{k}}}c_{k}\right).

This completes the proof of Theorem
1
.

Proof of Theorem
2
.
First, by the definition of
s_{\text{-}k,m}
, we have
\lambda_{j}\left(\frac{\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{k,m}\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{k,m}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}{T}\right)\asymp s_{\text{-}k,m}
for
j\in[r_{k}]
and for each
m
. Then,

\displaystyle\lambda_{1}\left(\frac{1}{M}\sum_{m=1}^{M}\frac{\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{k,m}\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{k,m}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}{T}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{M}\sum_{m=1}^{M}\lambda_{1}\left(\frac{\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{k,m}\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{k,m}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}{T}\right)\asymp s_{\text{-}k,pre},

\displaystyle\lambda_{r_{k}}\left(\frac{1}{M}\sum_{m=1}^{M}\frac{\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{k,m}\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{k,m}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}{T}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{M}\sum_{m=1}^{M}\lambda_{r_{k}}\left(\frac{\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{k,m}\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{k,m}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}{T}\right)\asymp s_{\text{-}k,pre}.

Therefore, for
j\in[r_{k}]
,

\displaystyle\lambda_{j}\left(\frac{1}{M}\sum_{m=1}^{M}\frac{\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{k,m}\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{k,m}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}{T}\right)\asymp s_{\text{-}k,pre}.

Next, following the same analysis as in the proof of Lemma
2
, it is easy to see that

\displaystyle\lambda_{j}\left(\frac{1}{M}\sum_{m=1}^{M}\frac{\mathbf{A}_{k}\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{k,m}\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{k,m}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{A}_{k}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}{T}\right)\asymp s_{\text{-}k,pre}\asymp\lambda_{j}\left(\frac{1}{M}\sum_{m=1}^{M}\frac{\mathbf{A}_{k}\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{k,m}\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}-\frac{1}{T}\mathbf{1}_{T}\mathbf{1}_{T}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\right)\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{k,m}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{A}_{k}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}{T}\right),

for
j\in[r_{k}]
. On the other hand, by Lemma
1
,

\displaystyle\lambda_{1}\left(\frac{1}{M}\sum_{m=1}^{M}\frac{\widetilde{\mathbf{E}}_{k,m}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\widetilde{\mathbf{E}}_{k,m}}{T}\right)\leq\frac{1}{M}\sum_{m=1}^{M}\lambda_{1}\left(\frac{\widetilde{\mathbf{E}}_{k,m}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\widetilde{\mathbf{E}}_{k,m}}{T}\right)=O_{p}\left(d_{\text{-}k,pre}\left(1+\frac{d_{k}}{T}\right)\right),

where
d_{\text{-}k,pre}=\frac{1}{M}\sum_{m=1}^{M}d_{\text{-}k,m}
. Similarly,

\displaystyle\lambda_{1}\left(\frac{1}{M}\sum_{m=1}^{M}\frac{\widetilde{\mathbf{E}}_{k,m}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}-\frac{1}{T}\mathbf{1}_{T}\mathbf{1}_{T}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\right)\widetilde{\mathbf{E}}_{k,m}}{T}\right)\leq O_{p}\left(d_{\text{-}k,pre}\left(1+\frac{d_{k}}{T}\right)\right).

If Assumptions (E1) – (R1) are satisfied for all
M
chosen samples, then
\frac{d_{\text{-}k,m}}{s_{\text{-}k,m}}\left(1+\frac{d_{k}}{T}\right)=o\left(d_{k}^{\alpha_{k,z_{k}}}\right)
for all
m
, which implies
\frac{d_{\text{-}k,pre}}{s_{\text{-}k,pre}}\left(1+\frac{d_{k}}{T}\right)=o\left(d_{k}^{\alpha_{k,z_{k}}}\right)
. Therefore, if we define the new normalized version
\ddot{\mathbf{X}}_{k,m}=\widetilde{\mathbf{X}}_{k,m}/s_{\text{-}k,pre}^{\frac{1}{2}}
, and
\ddot{\mathbf{F}}_{k,m}
and
\ddot{\mathbf{E}}_{k,m}
similarly, then following (
8
) and (
8
), (we can write
\frac{1}{M}\sum_{m=1}^{M}\frac{\ddot{\mathbf{X}}_{k,m}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}-\frac{1}{T}\mathbf{1}_{T}\mathbf{1}_{T}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\right)\ddot{\mathbf{X}}_{k,m}}{T}=\mathbf{X}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{X}
by Cholesky decomposition, and similarly define
\mathbf{F}
and
\mathbf{E}
, so that
\mathbf{X}=\mathbf{F}+\mathbf{E}
.) we have

\displaystyle\lambda_{j}\left(\frac{1}{M}\sum_{m=1}^{M}\frac{\ddot{\mathbf{X}}_{k,m}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}-\frac{1}{T}\mathbf{1}_{T}\mathbf{1}_{T}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\right)\ddot{\mathbf{X}}_{k,m}}{T}\right)\asymp 1

for
j\in[r_{k}]
. Then, we can make use of the decomposition

\displaystyle\frac{1}{M}\sum_{m=1}^{M}\frac{\ddot{\mathbf{X}}_{k,m}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}-\frac{1}{T}\mathbf{1}_{T}\mathbf{1}_{T}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\right)\ddot{\mathbf{X}}_{k,m}}{T}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{M}\sum_{m=1}^{M}\frac{\left(\mathbf{A}_{k}\ddot{\mathbf{F}}_{k,m}+\ddot{\mathbf{E}}_{k,m}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\right)\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}-\frac{1}{T}\mathbf{1}_{T}\mathbf{1}_{T}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\right)\left(\mathbf{A}_{k}\ddot{\mathbf{F}}_{k,m}+\ddot{\mathbf{E}}_{k,m}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\right)^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}{T}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{A}_{k}\left[\frac{1}{M}\sum_{m=1}^{M}\frac{\ddot{\mathbf{F}}_{k,m}\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}-\frac{1}{T}\mathbf{1}_{T}\mathbf{1}_{T}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\right)\ddot{\mathbf{F}}_{k,m}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}{T}\right]\mathbf{A}_{k}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}+R_{k,pre},

(8.38)

where

\displaystyle{}R_{k,pre}=\frac{1}{M}\sum_{m=1}^{M}\bigg[\frac{\mathbf{A}_{k}\ddot{\mathbf{F}}_{k,m}\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}-\frac{1}{T}\mathbf{1}_{T}\mathbf{1}_{T}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\right)\ddot{\mathbf{E}}_{k,m}}{T}

\displaystyle+\frac{\ddot{\mathbf{E}}_{k,m}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}-\frac{1}{T}\mathbf{1}_{T}\mathbf{1}_{T}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\right)\ddot{\mathbf{F}}_{k,m}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{A}_{k}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}{T}

\displaystyle+\frac{\ddot{\mathbf{E}}_{k,m}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}-\frac{1}{T}\mathbf{1}_{T}\mathbf{1}_{T}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\right)\ddot{\mathbf{E}}_{k,m}}{T}\bigg].

(8.39)

Then, following similar argument as in the proof of Theorem
1
, we can apply Lemma 3 of
Lam et al., 2011
and know that there exists
\widehat{\mathbf{U}}_{k,pre,(z_{k})}
(with
\widehat{\mathbf{U}}_{k,pre,(z_{k})}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\widehat{\mathbf{U}}_{k,pre,(z_{k})}=\mathbf{I}_{z_{k}}
) such that
\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{k,pre,(z_{k})}=\widehat{\mathbf{U}}_{k,pre,(z_{k})}\mathbf{P}_{k,pre,(z_{k})}
where
\mathbf{P}_{k,pre,(z_{k})}
is an orthogonal matrix, so that

\displaystyle\big\|\widehat{\mathbf{U}}_{k,pre,(z_{k})}-\mathbf{U}_{k,(z_{k})}\big\|

\displaystyle\preceq\frac{d_{k}^{\alpha_{k,1}}\bigg\|\frac{1}{M}\sum_{m=1}^{M}\frac{\ddot{\mathbf{F}}_{k,m}\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}-\frac{1}{T}\mathbf{1}_{T}\mathbf{1}_{T}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\right)\ddot{\mathbf{F}}_{k,m}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}{T}-\mathbf{I}_{r_{k}}\bigg\|+\big\|R_{k,pre}\big\|}{d_{k}^{\alpha_{k,z_{k}}}}.

To proceed, note that by (
8.26
), we have
\bigg\|\frac{\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{k,m}\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}-\frac{1}{T}\mathbf{1}_{T}\mathbf{1}_{T}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\right)\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{k,m}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}{Ts_{\text{-}k,m}}-\mathbf{I}_{r_{k}}\bigg\|=O_{p}\left(\sqrt{r_{k}/T}\right)
for each
m
, so
\bigg\|\frac{\ddot{\mathbf{F}}_{k,m}\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}-\frac{1}{T}\mathbf{1}_{T}\mathbf{1}_{T}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\right)\ddot{\mathbf{F}}_{k,m}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}{T}-\frac{s_{\text{-}k,m}}{s_{\text{-}k,pre}}\mathbf{I}_{r_{k}}\bigg\|=O_{p}\left(\frac{s_{\text{-}k,m}}{s_{\text{-}k,pre}}\sqrt{\frac{r_{k}}{T}}\right)
, and thus,

\displaystyle\bigg\|\frac{1}{M}\sum_{m=1}^{M}\frac{\ddot{\mathbf{F}}_{k}\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}-\frac{1}{T}\mathbf{1}_{T}\mathbf{1}_{T}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\right)\ddot{\mathbf{F}}_{k}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}{T}-\mathbf{I}_{r_{k}}\bigg\|

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{M}\bigg\|\sum_{m=1}^{M}\frac{\ddot{\mathbf{F}}_{k}\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}-\frac{1}{T}\mathbf{1}_{T}\mathbf{1}_{T}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\right)\ddot{\mathbf{F}}_{k}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}{T}-M\mathbf{I}_{r_{k}}\bigg\|

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{M}\bigg\|\sum_{m=1}^{M}\left[\frac{\ddot{\mathbf{F}}_{k,m}\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}-\frac{1}{T}\mathbf{1}_{T}\mathbf{1}_{T}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\right)\ddot{\mathbf{F}}_{k,m}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}{T}-\frac{s_{\text{-}k,m}}{s_{\text{-}k,pre}}\mathbf{I}_{r_{k}}\right]\bigg\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{M}\sum_{m=1}^{M}\bigg\|\frac{\ddot{\mathbf{F}}_{k,m}\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}-\frac{1}{T}\mathbf{1}_{T}\mathbf{1}_{T}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\right)\ddot{\mathbf{F}}_{k,m}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}{T}-\frac{s_{\text{-}k,m}}{s_{\text{-}k,pre}}\mathbf{I}_{r_{k}}\bigg\|

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{M}\sum_{m=1}^{M}O_{p}\left(\frac{s_{\text{-}k,m}}{s_{\text{-}k,pre}}\sqrt{\frac{r_{k}}{T}}\right)=O_{p}\left(\sqrt{\frac{r_{k}}{T}}\right).

To bound
\big\|R_{k,pre}\big\|^{2}
, we can bound each term on the right hand side of (
8
) by using the similar technique as in the proof of Theorem
1
. We have that

\displaystyle\bigg\|\frac{1}{M}\sum_{m=1}^{M}\frac{\mathbf{A}_{k}\ddot{\mathbf{F}}_{k,m}\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}-\frac{1}{T}\mathbf{1}_{T}\mathbf{1}_{T}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\right)\ddot{\mathbf{E}}_{k,m}}{T}\bigg\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{M}\sum_{m=1}^{M}\bigg\|\frac{\mathbf{A}_{k}\ddot{\mathbf{F}}_{k,m}\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}-\frac{1}{T}\mathbf{1}_{T}\mathbf{1}_{T}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\right)\ddot{\mathbf{E}}_{k,m}}{T}\bigg\|^{2}

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(d_{k}^{\alpha_{k,1}}\min{\left\{1+\frac{d_{k}}{T},\frac{r_{k}d_{k}}{T}\right\}}\frac{\frac{1}{M}\sum_{m=1}^{M}d_{\text{-}k,m}s_{\text{-}k,m}}{s_{\text{-}k,pre}^{2}}\right),

and similarly,

\displaystyle\bigg\|\frac{1}{M}\sum_{m=1}^{M}\frac{\ddot{\mathbf{E}}_{k,m}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}-\frac{1}{T}\mathbf{1}_{T}\mathbf{1}_{T}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\right)\ddot{\mathbf{F}}_{k,m}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{A}_{k}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}{T}\bigg\|^{2}=O_{p}\left(d_{k}^{\alpha_{k,1}}\min{\left\{1+\frac{d_{k}}{T},\frac{r_{k}d_{k}}{T}\right\}}\frac{\frac{1}{M}\sum_{m=1}^{M}d_{\text{-}k,m}s_{\text{-}k,m}}{s_{\text{-}k,pre}^{2}}\right),

and

\displaystyle\bigg\|\frac{1}{M}\sum_{m=1}^{M}\frac{\ddot{\mathbf{E}}_{k,m}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}-\frac{1}{T}\mathbf{1}_{T}\mathbf{1}_{T}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\right)\ddot{\mathbf{E}}_{k,m}}{T}\bigg\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{M}\sum_{m=1}^{M}\bigg\|\frac{\ddot{\mathbf{E}}_{k,m}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\left(\mathbf{I}_{T}-\frac{1}{T}\mathbf{1}_{T}\mathbf{1}_{T}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\right)\ddot{\mathbf{E}}_{k,m}}{T}\bigg\|^{2}

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\left(1+\frac{d_{k}^{2}}{T^{2}}\right)\frac{\frac{1}{M}\sum_{m=1}^{M}{d_{\text{-}k,m}^{2}}}{s_{\text{-}k,pre}^{2}}\right).

Combining the above results give

\displaystyle c_{k,pre}:=\big\|R_{k,pre}\big\|^{2}=O_{p}\bigg(\min{\left\{1+\frac{d_{k}}{T},\frac{r_{k}d_{k}}{T}\right\}}\frac{\frac{1}{M}\sum_{m=1}^{M}d_{\text{-}k,m}s_{\text{-}k,m}}{s_{\text{-}k,pre}^{2}}+d_{k}^{\alpha_{k,1}}\left(1+\frac{d_{k}^{2}}{T^{2}}\right)\frac{\frac{1}{M}\sum_{m=1}^{M}{d_{\text{-}k,m}^{2}}}{s_{\text{-}k,pre}^{2}}\bigg),

and thus,

\displaystyle\bigg\|\widehat{\mathbf{U}}_{k,pre,(z_{k})}-\mathbf{U}_{k,(z_{k})}\bigg\|^{2}

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(d_{k}^{-2\alpha_{k,z_{k}}}\left[d_{k}^{2\alpha_{k,1}}\frac{r_{k}}{T}+c_{k,pre}\right]\right).

Finally, to show (
3.15
), let
\widetilde{\mathbf{V}}_{k,pre}
be the
z_{k}\times z_{k}
diagonal matrix of the first
z_{k}
largest eigenvalues of
\widehat{\Sigma}_{\widetilde{\mathbf{x}}_{k,agg}}
in decreasing order, and
\ddot{\mathbf{V}}_{k,pre}:=\widetilde{\mathbf{V}}_{k,pre}/s_{\text{-}k,pre}
. Then it follows from (
8
) that

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{k,pre,(z_{k})}-\mathbf{Q}_{k}\ddot{\mathbf{H}}_{k,pre}

\displaystyle=R_{k,pre}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{k,pre,(z_{k})}\ddot{\mathbf{V}}_{k,pre}^{-1},\text{ implying }

\displaystyle\big\|\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{k,pre,(z_{k})}-\mathbf{Q}_{k}\ddot{\mathbf{H}}_{k,pre}\big\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\big\|R_{k,pre}\big\|^{2}\big\|\ddot{\mathbf{V}}_{k,pre}^{-1}\big\|^{2}=O_{p}\left(d_{k}^{-2\alpha_{k,z_{k}}}c_{k,pre}\right).

This completes the proof of Theorem
2
.
\square

Before the proof of Theorem
6
, we decompose

\displaystyle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}_{y,i}^{(k)}

\displaystyle=T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{y}_{t,i}^{(k)}\mathbf{y}_{t,i}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}=T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\text{mat}_{k}(\mathcal{X}_{t}-\bar{\mathcal{X}})\check{\mathbf{q}}_{\text{-}k}^{(i-1)}\check{\mathbf{q}}_{\text{-}k}^{(i-1){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}\text{mat}_{k}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}(\mathcal{X}_{t}-\bar{\mathcal{X}})

\displaystyle=T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(\mathbf{A}_{k}\text{mat}_{k}(\mathcal{F}_{t}-\bar{\mathcal{F}})\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\check{\mathbf{q}}_{\text{-}k}^{(i-1)}+\text{mat}_{k}(\mathcal{E}_{t}-\bar{\mathcal{E}})\check{\mathbf{q}}_{\text{-}k}^{(i-1)}\big)^{\otimes 2}

\displaystyle=T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\bigg\{\mathbf{A}_{k}\text{mat}_{k}(\mathcal{F}_{t}-\bar{\mathcal{F}})\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\check{\mathbf{q}}_{\text{-}k}^{(i-1)}+\big[\boldsymbol{\Psi}_{1}^{(k)}(\mathbf{e}_{t}^{(k)}-\bar{\mathbf{e}}^{(k)}),\ldots,\boldsymbol{\Psi}_{d_{\text{-}k}}^{(k)}(\mathbf{e}_{t}^{(k)}-\bar{\mathbf{e}}^{(k)})\big]\check{\mathbf{q}}_{\text{-}k}^{(i-1)}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad+\Big[\big(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\epsilon,1}^{(k)}\big)^{1/2}(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t,1}^{(k)}-\bar{\boldsymbol{\epsilon}}_{\cdot,1}^{(k)}),\ldots,\big(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\epsilon,d_{\text{-}k}}^{(k)}\big)^{1/2}(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t,d_{\text{-}k}}^{(k)}-\bar{\boldsymbol{\epsilon}}_{\cdot,d_{\text{-}k}}^{(k)})\Big]\check{\mathbf{q}}_{\text{-}k}^{(i-1)}\bigg\}^{\otimes 2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{3}\widetilde{\mathbf{S}}_{ii}+\sum_{i,j=1;i<j}^{3}(\widetilde{\mathbf{S}}_{ij}+\widetilde{\mathbf{S}}_{ij}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}),\text{ where }

\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathbf{S}}_{11}

\displaystyle:=\mathbf{A}_{k}(\mathbf{I}_{r_{k}}\otimes\check{\mathbf{q}}_{\text{-}k}^{(i-1){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k})T^{-1}\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}^{(k)}\mathbf{M}_{T}\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}(\mathbf{I}_{r_{k}}\otimes\check{\mathbf{q}}_{\text{-}k}^{(i-1){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k})^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{A}_{k}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}},

\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathbf{S}}_{12}

\displaystyle:=\mathbf{A}_{k}(\mathbf{I}_{r_{k}}\otimes\check{\mathbf{q}}_{\text{-}k}^{(i-1){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k})T^{-1}\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}^{(k)}\mathbf{M}_{T}\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\Theta}}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}\text{diag}^{1/2}(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\epsilon,1}^{(k)},\ldots,\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\epsilon,d_{\text{-}k}}^{(k)})(\check{\mathbf{q}}_{\text{-}k}^{(i-1){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}\otimes\mathbf{I}_{d_{k}})^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}},

\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathbf{S}}_{13}

\displaystyle:=(\mathbf{I}_{r_{k}}\otimes\check{\mathbf{q}}_{\text{-}k}^{(i-1){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k})T^{-1}\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}^{(k)}\mathbf{M}_{T}\widetilde{\mathbf{E}}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}\boldsymbol{\Psi}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}(\check{\mathbf{q}}_{\text{-}k}^{(i-1){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}\otimes\mathbf{I}_{d_{k}})^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}},

\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathbf{S}}_{22}

\displaystyle:=(\check{\mathbf{q}}_{\text{-}k}^{(i-1){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}\otimes\mathbf{I}_{d_{k}})\text{diag}^{1/2}(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\epsilon,1}^{(k)},\ldots,\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\epsilon,d_{\text{-}k}}^{(k)})T^{-1}\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\Theta}}^{(k)}\mathbf{M}_{T}\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\Theta}}^{(k)}\text{diag}^{1/2}(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\epsilon,1}^{(k)},\ldots,\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\epsilon,d_{\text{-}k}}^{(k)})(\check{\mathbf{q}}_{\text{-}k}^{(i-1){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}\otimes\mathbf{I}_{d_{k}})^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}},

\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathbf{S}}_{23}

\displaystyle:=(\check{\mathbf{q}}_{\text{-}k}^{(i-1){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}\otimes\mathbf{I}_{d_{k}})\text{diag}^{1/2}(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\epsilon,1}^{(k)},\ldots,\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\epsilon,d_{\text{-}k}}^{(k)})T^{-1}\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\Theta}}^{(k)}\mathbf{M}_{T}\widetilde{\mathbf{E}}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}\boldsymbol{\Psi}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}(\check{\mathbf{q}}_{\text{-}k}^{(i-1){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}\otimes\mathbf{I}_{d_{k}})^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}},

\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathbf{S}}_{33}

\displaystyle:=(\check{\mathbf{q}}_{\text{-}k}^{(i-1){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}\otimes\mathbf{I}_{d_{k}})\boldsymbol{\Psi}^{(k)}T^{-1}\widetilde{\mathbf{E}}^{(k)}\mathbf{M}_{T}\widetilde{\mathbf{E}}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}\boldsymbol{\Psi}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}(\check{\mathbf{q}}_{\text{-}k}^{(i-1){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}\otimes\mathbf{I}_{d_{k}})^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}},

with
\boldsymbol{\Psi}^{(k)}:=(\boldsymbol{\Psi}_{1}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}},\ldots,\boldsymbol{\Psi}_{d_{\text{-}k}}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}})^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}
,
\mathbf{M}_{T}=\mathbf{I}_{T}-T^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{T}\mathbf{1}_{T}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}
,

\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}^{(k)}

\displaystyle:=[\text{vec}(\text{mat}_{k}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}(\mathcal{F}_{1})),\ldots,\text{vec}(\text{mat}_{k}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}(\mathcal{F}_{T}))]=[\mathbf{f}_{1}^{(k)},\ldots,\mathbf{f}_{T}^{(k)}]\text{ with }

\displaystyle\mathbf{f}_{t}^{(k)}

\displaystyle:=(f_{t,1,1}^{(k)},\ldots,f_{t,r_{\text{-}k},1}^{(k)},\ldots,f_{t,1,r_{k}}^{(k)},\ldots,f_{t,r_{\text{-}k},r_{k}}^{(k)})^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}},

\displaystyle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\Theta}}^{(k)}

\displaystyle:=[\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{1}^{(k)},\ldots,\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{T}^{(k)}]\text{ with }\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t}^{(k)}:=(\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t,1}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}},\ldots,\boldsymbol{\epsilon}_{t,d_{\text{-}k}}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}})^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}},\text{ and }

\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathbf{E}}^{(k)}

\displaystyle:=[\mathbf{e}_{1}^{(k)},\ldots,\mathbf{e}_{T}^{(k)}].

With the notations in Assumption (E2) and (F1), for
t\in[T]
, define

\displaystyle\mathbf{z}_{e,t}^{(k)}

\displaystyle:=(z_{e,t,1}^{(k)},\ldots,z_{e,t,r_{e}}^{(k)})^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}},

\displaystyle\mathbf{z}_{\epsilon,t}^{(k)}

\displaystyle:=(z_{\epsilon,t,1,1}^{(k)},\ldots,z_{\epsilon,t,1,d_{k}}^{(k)},\ldots,z_{\epsilon,t,d_{\text{-}k},1}^{(k)},\ldots,z_{\epsilon,t,d_{\text{-}k},d_{k}}^{(k)})^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}},

\displaystyle\mathbf{z}_{f,t}^{(k)}

\displaystyle:=(z_{f,t,1,1}^{(k)},\ldots,z_{f,t,r_{\text{-}k},1}^{(k)},\ldots,z_{f,t,1,r_{k}}^{(k)},\ldots,z_{f,t,r_{\text{-}k},r_{k}}^{(k)})^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}.

We further split, for a fixed integer
N\geq 1
,

\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}^{(k)}

\displaystyle:=\mathbf{F}^{(k)}+\check{\mathbf{F}}^{(k)},\;\;\;\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\Theta}}^{(k)}:=\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{(k)}+\check{\boldsymbol{\Theta}}^{(k)},\;\;\;\widetilde{\mathbf{E}}^{(k)}:=\mathbf{E}^{(k)}+\check{\mathbf{E}}^{(k)},\;\text{ where }

\displaystyle\mathbf{F}^{(k)}

\displaystyle:=\bigg(\sum_{q=0}^{NT}a_{f,q}\mathbf{z}_{f,1-q}^{(k)},\ldots,\sum_{q=0}^{NT}a_{f,q}\mathbf{z}_{f,T-q}^{(k)}\bigg)=(\mathbf{z}_{f,1-NT}^{(k)},\ldots,\mathbf{z}_{f,T}^{(k)})\mathcal{A}_{f,T}=:\mathbf{Z}_{f}^{(k)}\mathcal{A}_{f,T},

\displaystyle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\Theta}}^{(k)}

\displaystyle:=\bigg(\sum_{q=0}^{NT}a_{\epsilon,q}\mathbf{z}_{\epsilon,1-q}^{(k)},\ldots,\sum_{q=0}^{NT}a_{\epsilon,q}\mathbf{z}_{\epsilon,T-q}^{(k)}\bigg)=(\mathbf{z}_{\epsilon,1-NT}^{(k)},\ldots,\mathbf{z}_{\epsilon,T}^{(k)})\mathcal{A}_{\epsilon,T}=:\mathbf{Z}_{\epsilon}^{(k)}\mathcal{A}_{\epsilon,T},

\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathbf{E}}^{(k)}

\displaystyle:=\bigg(\sum_{q=0}^{NT}a_{e,q}\mathbf{z}_{e,1-q}^{(k)},\ldots,\sum_{q=0}^{NT}a_{e,q}\mathbf{z}_{e,T-q}^{(k)}\bigg)=(\mathbf{z}_{e,1-NT}^{(k)},\ldots,\mathbf{z}_{e,T}^{(k)})\mathcal{A}_{e,T}=:\mathbf{Z}_{e}^{(k)}\mathcal{A}_{e,T},

with
\mathcal{A}_{f,T}
,
\mathcal{A}_{\epsilon,T}
and
\mathcal{A}_{e,T}
defined in Assumption (RE1), and
\check{\mathbf{F}}^{(k)}
,
\check{\boldsymbol{\Theta}}^{(k)}
and
\check{\mathbf{E}}^{(k)}
the remainders of the truncations. Then we define

\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathbf{S}}_{ij}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{S}_{ij}+\check{\mathbf{S}}_{ij},\;\text{ where }

\displaystyle\mathbf{S}_{11}

\displaystyle:=\mathbf{A}_{k}(\mathbf{I}_{r_{k}}\otimes\check{\mathbf{q}}_{\text{-}k}^{(i-1){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k})T^{-1}\mathbf{F}^{(k)}\mathbf{M}_{T}\mathbf{F}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}(\mathbf{I}_{r_{k}}\otimes\check{\mathbf{q}}_{\text{-}k}^{(i-1){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k})^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{A}_{k}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}},

(8.40)

and similarly for other
\mathbf{S}_{ij}
’s. We first prove a lemma on how small each remainder term
\check{\mathbf{S}}_{ij}
is.

Lemma 4
.

Let all the assumptions in Theorem
6
be satisfied. Then for each
k\in[K]
,

\sum_{i,j=1;i\leq j}^{3}\big\|\check{\mathbf{S}}_{ij}\big\|=o_{P}(T^{-1}).

Proof of Lemma
4
.

Define

\begin{split}\mathbf{Q}_{1}^{(k)}&:=\mathbf{A}_{k}(\mathbf{I}_{r_{k}}\otimes\check{\mathbf{q}}_{\text{-}k}^{(i-1){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k}),\\
\mathbf{Q}_{2}^{(k)}&:=(\check{\mathbf{q}}_{\text{-}k}^{(i-1){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}\otimes\mathbf{I}_{d_{k}})\text{diag}^{1/2}(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\epsilon,1}^{(k)},\ldots,\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\epsilon,d_{\text{-}k}}^{(k)}),\\
\mathbf{Q}_{3}^{(k)}&:=(\check{\mathbf{q}}_{\text{-}k}^{(i-1){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}\otimes\mathbf{I}_{d_{k}})\boldsymbol{\Psi}^{(k)}.\end{split}

(8.41)

Then by Assumption (L1) and the rates for
\max_{j\in[d_{\text{-}k}]}\big\|\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\epsilon,j}^{(k)}\big\|
and
S_{\psi}^{(k)}
in Assumption (RE2),

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{Q}_{1}^{(k)}\big\|^{2}

\displaystyle=(\check{\mathbf{q}}_{\text{-}k}^{(i-1){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k}\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\check{\mathbf{q}}_{\text{-}k}^{(i-1)})\big\|\mathbf{A}_{k}\big\|^{2}=O\bigg(\prod_{j=1;j\neq k}^{K}d_{j}^{\alpha_{j,1}}\cdot d_{k}^{\alpha_{k,1}}\bigg)=o(d),

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{Q}_{2}^{(k)}\big\|^{2}

\displaystyle=O\Big(\max_{j\in[d_{\text{-}k}]}\big\|\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\epsilon,j}^{(k)}\big\|\Big)=O(d_{\text{-}k})=o(d),\;\;\;\big\|\mathbf{Q}_{3}^{(k)}\big\|^{2}=O(S_{\psi}^{(k)})=O(d),

where the first line used (
5.3
). We also have, by Assumption (RE1),

\displaystyle E\big\|T^{-1/2}\mathbf{F}^{(k)}\big\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle=T^{-1}E\text{tr}(\mathcal{A}_{f,T}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{Z}_{f}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}\mathbf{Z}_{f}^{(k)}\mathcal{A}_{f,T})=rT^{-1}\text{tr}(\mathcal{A}_{f,T}\mathcal{A}_{f,T}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}})=r(1-o(T^{-2}d^{-4})),

\displaystyle E\big\|T^{-1/2}\check{\mathbf{F}}^{(k)}\big\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle=O(rT\cdot T^{-1}\cdot o(T^{-2}d^{-4})=o(rT^{-2}d^{-4})),

\displaystyle E\big\|T^{-1/2}\boldsymbol{\Theta}^{(k)}\big\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle=d(1-o(T^{-2}d^{-4})),\;\;\;E\big\|T^{-1/2}\check{\boldsymbol{\Theta}}^{(k)}\big\|_{F}^{2}=o(dT^{-2}d^{-4})=o(T^{-2}d^{-3}),

\displaystyle E\big\|T^{-1/2}\mathbf{E}^{(k)}\big\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle=r_{e}(1-o(T^{-2}d^{-4})),\;\;\;E\big\|T^{-1/2}\check{\mathbf{E}}^{(k)}\big\|_{F}^{2}=o(r_{e}T^{-2}d^{-4}).

Then writing
\check{\mathbf{S}}_{11}=I_{1}+I_{1}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}+I_{2}
, where

\displaystyle I_{1}

\displaystyle:=T^{-1}\mathbf{Q}_{1}^{(k)}\check{\mathbf{F}}^{(k)}\mathbf{M}_{T}\mathbf{F}^{(k)}\mathbf{Q}_{1}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}},\;\;\;I_{2}:=T^{-1}\mathbf{Q}_{1}^{(k)}\check{\mathbf{F}}^{(k)}\mathbf{M}_{T}\check{\mathbf{F}}^{(k)}\mathbf{Q}_{1}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}},

we have for
a>0
,

\displaystyle P(\big\|I_{1}\big\|\geq a)

\displaystyle\leq\big\|\mathbf{Q}_{1}^{(k)}\big\|^{2}E^{1/2}\big\|T^{-1/2}\mathbf{F}^{(k)}\big\|_{F}^{2}\cdot E^{1/2}\big\|T^{-1/2}\check{\mathbf{F}}^{(k)}\big\|_{F}^{2}/a

\displaystyle=o(d\cdot r^{1/2}\cdot r^{1/2}T^{-2/2}d^{-4/2}/a)=o(rT^{-1}d^{-1}/a),

showing that
\big\|I_{1}\big\|=o_{P}(rT^{-1}d^{-1})
. Similarly (details omitted),

\big\|I_{2}\big\|=O_{P}(rT^{-2}d^{-3})
, so that
\big\|\check{\mathbf{S}}_{11}\big\|=o_{P}(rT^{-1}d^{-1})
. Similar to the above arguments, we can show that (details omitted)

\displaystyle\big\|\check{\mathbf{S}}_{12}\big\|

\displaystyle=o_{P}(r^{1/2}T^{-1}d^{-1/2}),\;\;\;\big\|\check{\mathbf{S}}_{13}\big\|=o_{P}(r^{1/2}r_{e}^{1/2}T^{-1}d^{-1}),\;\;\;\big\|\check{\mathbf{S}}_{22}\big\|=o_{P}(T^{-1}),

\displaystyle\big\|\check{\mathbf{S}}_{23}\big\|

\displaystyle=o_{P}(r_{e}^{1/2}T^{-1/2}d^{-1/2}),\;\;\;\big\|\check{\mathbf{S}}_{33}\big\|=o_{P}(r_{e}T^{-1}d^{-1}).

The proof completes by noting that
r_{e}=O(d_{\text{-}k})=o(d)
by (RE2), and
r=O(r_{e})
by the assumption in Theorem
6
.
\square

Before presenting the next lemma, define, for
j\in[d_{\text{-}k}]
and
k\in[K]
, and
m
a non-negative integer,

\displaystyle\mathbf{S}_{11,m}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=(\check{\mathbf{q}}_{\text{-}k}^{(m){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k}\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\check{\mathbf{q}}_{\text{-}k}^{(m)})\mathbf{A}_{k}\mathbf{A}_{k}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}},

\displaystyle\mathbf{S}_{11,m}^{\prime\prime}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{A}_{k}(\mathbf{I}_{r_{k}}\otimes\check{\mathbf{q}}_{\text{-}k}^{(m){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k})(T^{-1}\mathbf{F}^{(k)}\mathbf{M}_{T}\mathbf{F}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}-\mathbf{I}_{r})(\mathbf{I}_{r_{k}}\otimes\check{\mathbf{q}}_{\text{-}k}^{(m){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k})^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{A}_{k}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}},

so that
\mathbf{S}_{11}=\mathbf{S}_{11,i-1}^{\prime}+\mathbf{S}_{11,i-1}^{\prime\prime}
, where
\mathbf{S}_{11}
is defined in (
8.40
). Define
\mathbf{S}_{ij,m}:=\mathbf{S}_{ij}
in the spirit of (
8.40
) for
i,j=1,2,3
, with
\check{\mathbf{q}}_{\text{-}k}^{(i-1)}
there replaced by
\check{\mathbf{q}}_{\text{-}k}^{(m)}
. Then we have
\mathbf{S}_{11,m}=\mathbf{S}_{11,m}^{\prime}+\mathbf{S}_{11,m}^{\prime\prime}
, and

\displaystyle\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}_{y,m+1}^{(k)}

\displaystyle=T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{y}_{t,m+1}^{(k)}\mathbf{y}_{t,m+1}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}=\mathbf{S}_{11,m}^{\prime}+\mathbf{S}_{11,m}^{\prime\prime}+\mathbf{S}_{22,m}+\mathbf{S}_{33,m}+\sum_{i,j=1;i<j}^{3}(\mathbf{S}_{ij,m}+\mathbf{S}_{ij,m}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}})+\sum_{i,j=1}^{3}\check{\mathbf{S}}_{ij}

\displaystyle=:\mathbf{S}_{11,m}^{\prime}+\mathbf{E}.

(8.42)

Lemma 5
.

Let all the assumptions in Theorem
6
be satisfied. Then for
j\in[d_{\text{-}k}]
and
k\in[K]
, we have

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{S}_{11,m}^{\prime\prime}\big\|

\displaystyle=O_{P}\bigg(\check{g}_{\text{-}k}d_{k}^{\alpha_{k,1}}\sqrt{\frac{r}{T}}\bigg),\;\;\;\big\|\mathbf{S}_{12,m}\big\|=O_{P}\bigg\{\check{g}_{\text{-}k}^{1/2}d_{k}^{\alpha_{k,1}/2}\bigg(\sqrt{\frac{rd_{k}}{T}}+\big\|\check{\mathbf{q}}_{\text{-}k}^{(m)}-\mathbf{U}_{\text{-}k,(1)}\big\|\sqrt{\frac{rd}{T}}\bigg)\bigg\},

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{S}_{13,m}\big\|

\displaystyle=O_{P}\bigg(\check{g}_{\text{-}k}^{1/2}d_{k}^{\alpha_{k,1}/2}\sqrt{\frac{rr_{e}S_{\psi}^{(k)}}{T}}\bigg),

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{S}_{22,m}\big\|

\displaystyle=O_{P}\bigg(\max_{j\in[d_{\text{-}k}]}\big\|\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\epsilon,j}^{(k)}\big\|\Big[1+\big\|\check{\mathbf{q}}_{\text{-}k}^{(m)}-\mathbf{U}_{\text{-}k,(1)}\big\|^{2}\frac{d}{T}\Big]+\frac{d_{k}}{\sqrt{T}}+\big\|\check{\mathbf{q}}_{\text{-}k}^{(m)}-\mathbf{U}_{\text{-}k,(1)}\big\|\sqrt{\frac{d_{k}d}{T}}\bigg),

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{S}_{23,m}\big\|

\displaystyle=O_{P}\bigg\{(S_{\psi}^{(k)})^{1/2}\bigg(\sqrt{\frac{r_{e}d_{k}}{T}}+\big\|\check{\mathbf{q}}_{\text{-}k}^{(m)}-\mathbf{U}_{\text{-}k,(1)}\big\|\sqrt{\frac{r_{e}d}{T}}\bigg)\bigg\},\;\;\;\big\|\mathbf{S}_{33,m}\big\|=O_{P}(S_{\psi}^{(k)}),

where
\mathbf{U}_{\text{-}k,(1)}:=\mathbf{U}_{K,(1)}\otimes\cdots\otimes\mathbf{U}_{k+1,(1)}\otimes\mathbf{U}_{k-1,(1)}\otimes\cdots\otimes\mathbf{U}_{1,(1)}
.

Proof of Lemma
5
.
Using (
5.3
), (
3.8
) and that (
8.6
) proves that
\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{G}_{k})\asymp d_{k}^{\alpha_{k,j}}
for
j\in[r_{k}]
, we have

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{S}_{11,m}^{\prime\prime}\big\|

\displaystyle\leq(\check{\mathbf{q}}_{\text{-}k}^{(m){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k}\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\check{\mathbf{q}}_{\text{-}k}^{(m)})\big\|\mathbf{A}_{k}\big\|^{2}\cdot\big\|T^{-1}\mathbf{Z}_{f}^{(k)}\mathcal{A}_{f,T}\mathbf{M}_{T}\mathcal{A}_{f,T}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{Z}_{f}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}-\mathbf{I}_{r}\big\|

\displaystyle=\check{g}_{\text{-}k}\big\|\mathbf{G}_{k}\big\|\cdot\big\|T^{-1}\mathbf{Z}_{f}^{(k)}\mathcal{A}_{f,T}\mathbf{M}_{T}\mathcal{A}_{f,T}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{Z}_{f}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}-\mathbf{I}_{r}\big\|=O_{P}\bigg(\check{g}_{\text{-}k}d_{k}^{\alpha_{k,1}}\sqrt{\frac{r}{T}}\bigg),

where the last equality used Theorem 2.8 of
Wang and Paul, 2014
, which can be applied since we have
r_{k}=o(T^{1/3})
and with fourth order moments exist for the elements in
\mathbf{Z}_{f}^{(k)}
from Assumption (L1) and (R1) respectively, and that by Assumption (RE1),

\displaystyle\big\|\mathcal{A}_{f,T}\mathbf{M}_{T}\mathcal{A}_{f,T}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\big\|

\displaystyle\leq\big\|\mathbf{A}_{f,T}\big\|^{2}<\infty,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{(N+1)T}\text{tr}(\mathcal{A}_{f,T}\mathbf{M}_{T}\mathcal{A}_{f,T}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{(N+1)T}(\text{tr}(\mathcal{A}_{f,T}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathcal{A}_{f,T})-T^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{T}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathcal{A}_{f,T}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathcal{A}_{f,T}\mathbf{1}_{T})\rightarrow\frac{1}{N+1},

\displaystyle\frac{1}{(N+1)T}\text{tr}(\mathcal{A}_{f,T}\mathbf{M}_{T}\mathcal{A}_{f,T}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}})^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{(N+1)T}\big\{\text{tr}(\mathcal{A}_{f,T}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathcal{A}_{f,T})^{2}-2T^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{T}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}(\mathcal{A}_{f,T}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathcal{A}_{f,T})^{2}\mathbf{1}_{T}

\displaystyle\hskip 57.81621pt+T^{-2}(\mathbf{1}_{T}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathcal{A}_{f,T}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathcal{A}_{f,T}\mathbf{1}_{T})^{2}\big\}\rightarrow\frac{a_{1}-2a_{2}+a_{3}^{2}}{N+1}.

For
\big\|\mathbf{S}_{12,m}\big\|
, define the notation
\mathbf{U}_{S}
to be a sub-matrix of
\mathbf{U}
restricted to the rows indexed by
S
. Let
\mathbf{Z}_{j}:=(\mathbf{Z}_{\epsilon}^{(k)})_{B_{j}}\in\mathbb{R}^{d_{k}\times(M+1)T}
,
where
B_{j}:=\{{(j-1)d_{k}+1,\ldots,jd_{k}}\}
,
j=1,\ldots,d_{\text{-}k}
. Let also
\mathbf{a}_{\ell,j}:=(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\epsilon,j}^{(k)})_{\ell}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}
,
\ell\in[d_{k}]
. Using the notation in (
8.41
), for
h\in[r]
and
\ell\in[d_{k}]
, the
(h,\ell)
-th element of
T^{-1}\mathbf{Z}_{f}^{(k)}\mathcal{A}_{f,T}\mathbf{M}_{T}\mathcal{A}_{\epsilon,T}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{Z}_{\epsilon}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}\mathbf{Q}_{2}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}
is
J_{1}+J_{2}
, where

\displaystyle J_{1}

\displaystyle:=T^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}(\mathbf{U}_{\text{-}k,(1)})_{j}\mathbf{a}_{\ell,j}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{Z}_{j}\mathcal{A}_{f,T}\mathbf{M}_{T}\mathcal{A}_{\epsilon,T}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}(\mathbf{Z}_{f}^{(k)})_{h},

\displaystyle J_{2}

\displaystyle:=T^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}(\check{\mathbf{q}}_{\text{-}k}^{(m)}-\mathbf{U}_{\text{-}k,(1)})_{j}\mathbf{a}_{\ell,j}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{Z}_{j}\mathcal{A}_{f,T}\mathbf{M}_{T}\mathcal{A}_{\epsilon,T}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}(\mathbf{Z}_{f}^{(k)})_{h}.

We have
EJ_{1}=0
, and by Assumption (E1),

\displaystyle E|J_{1}|^{2}

\displaystyle=T^{-2}\sum_{j=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}(\mathbf{U}_{\text{-}k,(1)})_{j}^{2}\big\|\mathbf{a}_{\ell,j}\big\|^{2}\big\|\mathcal{A}_{f,T}\mathbf{M}_{T}\mathcal{A}_{\epsilon,T}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\big\|_{F}^{2}\leq O(T^{-2})\cdot\big\|\mathcal{A}_{f,T}\big\|\big\|\mathbf{M}_{T}\big\|\cdot\big\|\mathcal{A}_{\epsilon,T}\big\|_{F}^{2}=O(T^{-1}),

where the last equality is from Assumption (RE1). It means that
J_{1}=O_{P}(T^{-1/2})
. For
J_{2}
, using the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality and that
\mathbf{a}_{\ell,j}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{Z}_{j}\mathcal{A}_{f,T}\mathbf{M}_{T}\mathcal{A}_{\epsilon,T}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}(\mathbf{Z}_{f}^{(k)})_{h}=O_{P}(T^{1/2})
from the analysis of
J_{2}
above,

\displaystyle J_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\big\|\check{\mathbf{q}}_{\text{-}k}^{(m)}-\mathbf{U}_{\text{-}k,(1)}\big\|\cdot T^{-1}\bigg(\sum_{j=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}(\mathbf{a}_{\ell,j}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{Z}_{j}\mathcal{A}_{f,T}\mathbf{M}_{T}\mathcal{A}_{\epsilon,T}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}(\mathbf{Z}_{f}^{(k)})_{h})^{2}\bigg)^{1/2}

\displaystyle=\big\|\check{\mathbf{q}}_{\text{-}k}^{(m)}-\mathbf{U}_{\text{-}k,(1)}\big\|\cdot T^{-1}O_{P}(d_{\text{-}k}T)^{1/2}=O_{P}\bigg(\big\|\check{\mathbf{q}}_{\text{-}k}^{(m)}-\mathbf{U}_{\text{-}k,(1)}\big\|\sqrt{\frac{d_{\text{-}k}}{T}}\bigg).

With the above, we then have

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{S}_{12,m}\big\|

\displaystyle=\big\|\mathbf{Q}_{1}^{(k)}T^{-1}\mathbf{Z}_{f}^{(k)}\mathcal{A}_{f,T}\mathbf{M}_{T}\mathcal{A}_{\epsilon,T}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{Z}_{\epsilon}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}\mathbf{Q}_{2}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}\big\|\leq\big\|\mathbf{Q}_{1}^{(k)}\big\|\cdot\sqrt{rd_{k}}\cdot O_{P}(J_{1}+J_{2})

\displaystyle=O_{P}\bigg\{\check{g}_{\text{-}k}^{1/2}d_{k}^{\alpha_{k,1}/2}\bigg(\sqrt{\frac{rd_{k}}{T}}+\big\|\check{\mathbf{q}}_{\text{-}k}^{(m)}-\mathbf{U}_{\text{-}k,(1)}\big\|\sqrt{\frac{rd}{T}}\bigg)\bigg\}.

For
\mathbf{S}_{13,m}
, using the definitions in (
8.41
) and the bounds for
\big\|\mathbf{Q}_{3}^{(k)}\big\|
in Lemma
4
,

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{S}_{13,m}\big\|

\displaystyle\leq\big\|\mathbf{Q}_{1}^{(k)}\big\|\cdot\big\|\mathbf{Q}_{3}^{(k)}\big\|\cdot\big\|T^{-1}\mathbf{Z}_{f}^{(k)}\mathcal{A}_{f,T}\mathbf{M}_{T}\mathcal{A}_{e,T}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{Z}_{e}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}\big\|

\displaystyle\leq\check{g}_{\text{-}k}^{1/2}d_{k}^{\alpha_{k,1}/2}\cdot(S_{\psi}^{(k)})^{1/2}\cdot O_{P}(T^{-1}\sqrt{rr_{e}}\big\|\mathcal{A}_{f,T}\mathbf{M}_{T}\mathcal{A}_{e,T}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\big\|_{F})=O_{P}\bigg(\check{g}_{\text{-}k}^{1/2}d_{k}^{\alpha_{k,1}/2}(S_{\psi}^{(k)})^{1/2}\sqrt{\frac{rr_{e}}{T}}\bigg),

where the last line used the fact that an element in
\mathbf{Z}_{f}^{(k)}\mathcal{A}_{f,T}\mathbf{M}_{T}\mathcal{A}_{e,T}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{Z}_{e}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}
is
O_{P}(\big\|\mathcal{A}_{f,T}\mathbf{M}_{T}\mathcal{A}_{e,T}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\big\|_{F})=O_{P}(T^{1/2})
by a similar calculation for treating
J_{1}
above.

For
\mathbf{S}_{22,m}
, decompose
\mathbf{Q}_{2}^{(k)}=\mathbf{Q}_{2,0}^{(k)}+\mathbf{Q}_{2,e}^{(k)}
, where

\mathbf{Q}_{2,0}^{(k)}:=(\mathbf{U}_{\text{-}k,(1)}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\otimes I_{d_{k}})\text{diag}^{1/2}(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\epsilon,1}^{(k)},\ldots,\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\epsilon,d_{\text{-}k}}^{(k)}),\;\;\;\mathbf{Q}_{2,e}^{(k)}:=((\check{\mathbf{q}}_{\text{-}k}^{(k)}-\mathbf{U}_{\text{-}k,(1)})^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\otimes I_{d_{k}})\text{diag}^{1/2}(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\epsilon,1}^{(k)},\ldots,\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\epsilon,d_{\text{-}k}}^{(k)}).

Then
\mathbf{S}_{22,m}=I_{0}+I_{1}+I_{2}+I_{2}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}+I_{3}
, where

\displaystyle I_{0}

\displaystyle:=\mathbf{Q}_{2}^{(k)}\mathbf{Q}_{2}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}},

\displaystyle I_{1}

\displaystyle:=\mathbf{Q}_{2,0}^{(k)}(T^{-1}\mathbf{Z}_{\epsilon}^{(k)}\mathcal{A}_{\epsilon,T}\mathbf{M}_{T}\mathcal{A}_{\epsilon,T}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{Z}_{\epsilon}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}-\mathbf{I}_{d})\mathbf{Q}_{2,0}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}},

\displaystyle I_{2}

\displaystyle:=\mathbf{Q}_{2,e}^{(k)}(T^{-1}\mathbf{Z}_{\epsilon}^{(k)}\mathcal{A}_{\epsilon,T}\mathbf{M}_{T}\mathcal{A}_{\epsilon,T}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{Z}_{\epsilon}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}-\mathbf{I}_{d})\mathbf{Q}_{2,0}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}},

\displaystyle I_{3}

\displaystyle:=\mathbf{Q}_{2,e}^{(k)}(T^{-1}\mathbf{Z}_{\epsilon}^{(k)}\mathcal{A}_{\epsilon,T}\mathbf{M}_{T}\mathcal{A}_{\epsilon,T}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{Z}_{\epsilon}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}-\mathbf{I}_{d})\mathbf{Q}_{2,e}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}.

Firstly,

\big\|I_{0}\big\|=\big\|\mathbf{Q}_{2}^{(k)}\big\|^{2}\leq\max_{j\in[d_{\text{-}k}]}\big\|\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\epsilon,j}^{(k)}\big\|.

Using the same notations as in the treatment of
\mathbf{S}_{12,m}
before within this proof, for
\ell,h\in[d_{k}]
, the
(\ell,h)
entry of
I_{1}
is given by

\displaystyle(I_{1})_{\ell,h}=T^{-1}\bigg(\sum_{j=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}(\mathbf{U}_{\text{-}k,(1)})_{j}\mathbf{a}_{\ell,j}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{Z}_{j}\bigg)\mathcal{A}_{\epsilon,T}\mathbf{M}_{T}\mathcal{A}_{\epsilon,T}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\bigg(\sum_{j=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}(\mathbf{U}_{\text{-}k,(1)})_{j}\mathbf{a}_{h,j}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{Z}_{j}\bigg)^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}-\sum_{j=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}(\mathbf{U}_{\text{-}k,(1)})_{j}^{2}\mathbf{a}_{\ell,j}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{a}_{h,j}.

Hence by Assumption (RE1) and (E1), writing
\mathbf{G}:=\mathcal{A}_{\epsilon,T}\mathbf{M}_{T}\mathcal{A}_{\epsilon,T}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}
,

\displaystyle E(I_{1})_{\ell,h}

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}(\mathbf{U}_{\text{-}k,(1)})_{j}^{2}\mathbf{a}_{\ell,j}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{a}_{h,j}\{T^{-1}\text{tr}(\mathbf{G})-1\}=O(T^{-1/2}).

Also,

\displaystyle E(I_{1})_{\ell,h}^{2}

\displaystyle=T^{-2}\sum_{i,j=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}(\mathbf{U}_{\text{-}k,(1)})_{i}^{2}(\mathbf{U}_{\text{-}k,(1)})_{j}^{2}E\{(\mathbf{a}_{\ell,i}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{Z}_{i}\mathbf{G}\mathbf{Z}_{i}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{a}_{h,i})(\mathbf{a}_{\ell,j}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{Z}_{j}\mathbf{G}\mathbf{Z}_{j}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{a}_{h,j})\}

\displaystyle+T^{-2}\sum_{i\neq j}(\mathbf{U}_{\text{-}k,(1)})_{i}^{2}(\mathbf{U}_{\text{-}k,(1)})_{j}^{2}E\{(\mathbf{a}_{\ell,i}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{Z}_{i}\mathbf{G}\mathbf{Z}_{j}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{a}_{h,j})^{2}+E\{\mathbf{a}_{\ell,i}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{Z}_{i}\mathbf{G}\mathbf{Z}_{j}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{a}_{h,j}\mathbf{a}_{\ell,j}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{Z}_{j}\mathbf{G}\mathbf{Z}_{i}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{a}_{h,i}\}\}

\displaystyle-2T^{-1}\text{tr}(\mathbf{G})\bigg(\sum_{j=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}(\mathbf{U}_{\text{-}k,(1)})_{j}^{2}\mathbf{a}_{\ell,j}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{a}_{h,j}\bigg)^{2}+\bigg(\sum_{j=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}(\mathbf{U}_{\text{-}k,(1)})_{j}^{2}\mathbf{a}_{\ell,j}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{a}_{h,j}\bigg)^{2}

\displaystyle=T^{-2}\sum_{i=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}(\mathbf{U}_{\text{-}k,(1)})_{i}^{4}E(\mathbf{a}_{\ell,i}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{Z}_{i}\mathbf{G}\mathbf{Z}_{i}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{a}_{h,i})^{2}+T^{-2}\sum_{i\neq j}(\mathbf{U}_{\text{-}k,(1)})_{i}^{2}(\mathbf{U}_{\text{-}k,(1)})_{j}^{2}(\mathbf{a}_{\ell,i}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{a}_{h,i})(\mathbf{a}_{\ell,j}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{a}_{h,j})\text{tr}^{2}(\mathbf{G})

\displaystyle+T^{-2}\sum_{i\neq j}(\mathbf{U}_{\text{-}k,(1)})_{i}^{2}(\mathbf{U}_{\text{-}k,(1)})_{j}^{2}(\big\|\mathbf{a}_{\ell,i}\big\|^{2}\big\|\mathbf{a}_{h,j}\big\|^{2}+(\mathbf{a}_{\ell,i}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{a}_{h,i})(\mathbf{a}_{\ell,j}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{a}_{h,j}))\text{tr}(\mathbf{G}^{2})

\displaystyle-(2T^{-1}\text{tr}(\mathbf{G})-1)\bigg(\sum_{j=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}(\mathbf{U}_{\text{-}k,(1)})_{j}^{2}\mathbf{a}_{\ell,j}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{a}_{h,j}\bigg)^{2}.

(8.43)

Now define
\mathbf{w}_{\ell}:=\mathbf{Z}_{i}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{a}_{\ell,i}
. Then
E(\mathbf{w}_{\ell})=\mathbf{0}
and
\text{cov}(\mathbf{w}_{\ell},\mathbf{w}_{h})=\mathbf{a}_{\ell,i}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{a}_{h,i}\mathbf{I}_{(M+1)T}
, with elements in
\mathbf{w}_{\ell}
independent of each other for each
\ell\in[d_{k}]
and
i\in[d_{\text{-}k}]
. Hence

\displaystyle E(\mathbf{a}_{\ell,i}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{Z}_{i}\mathbf{G}\mathbf{Z}_{i}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{a}_{h,i})^{2}

\displaystyle=E(\mathbf{w}_{\ell}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{G}\mathbf{w}_{h})=E\bigg(\sum_{j=1}^{(N+1)T}(\mathbf{G})_{jj}(\mathbf{w}_{\ell})_{j}(\mathbf{w}_{h})_{j}+\sum_{j_{1}\neq j_{2}}(\mathbf{G})_{j_{1}j_{2}}(\mathbf{w}_{\ell})_{j_{1}}(\mathbf{w}_{h})_{j_{2}}\bigg)^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{(N+1)T}(\mathbf{G})_{jj}^{2}E[(\mathbf{w}_{\ell})_{j}^{2}(\mathbf{w}_{h})_{j}^{2}]+\sum_{j_{1}\neq j_{2}}(\mathbf{G})_{j_{1}j_{1}}(\mathbf{G})_{j_{2}j_{2}}(\mathbf{a}_{\ell,i}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{a}_{h,i})^{2}

\displaystyle+\sum_{j_{1}\neq j_{2}}(\mathbf{G})_{j_{1}j_{2}}^{2}(\big\|\mathbf{a}_{\ell,i}\big\|^{2}\big\|\mathbf{a}_{h,i}\big\|^{2}+(\mathbf{a}_{\ell,i}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{a}_{h,i})^{2})

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{(N+1)T}(\mathbf{G})_{jj}^{2}\Big\{\big\|\mathbf{a}_{h,i}\big\|^{2}\big\|\mathbf{a}_{\ell,i}\big\|^{2}+(\nu_{4}-3)\mathbf{a}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\text{diag}(\mathbf{a}_{\ell,i}\mathbf{a}_{\ell,i}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}})\mathbf{a}_{h,i}+2(\mathbf{a}_{\ell,i}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{a}_{h,i})^{2}\Big\}

\displaystyle+\sum_{j_{1}\neq j_{2}}(\mathbf{G})_{j_{1}j_{1}}(\mathbf{G})_{j_{2}j_{2}}(\mathbf{a}_{\ell,i}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{a}_{h,i})^{2}+\sum_{j_{1}\neq j_{2}}(\mathbf{G})_{j_{1}j_{2}}^{2}(\big\|\mathbf{a}_{\ell,i}\big\|^{2}\big\|\mathbf{a}_{h,i}\big\|^{2}+(\mathbf{a}_{\ell,i}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{a}_{h,i})^{2})

\displaystyle=\big\|\mathbf{a}_{h,i}\big\|^{2}\big\|\mathbf{a}_{\ell,i}\big\|^{2}\text{tr}(\mathbf{G}^{2})+(\mathbf{a}_{\ell,i}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{a}_{h,i})^{2}(\text{tr}^{2}(\mathbf{G})+\text{tr}(\mathbf{G}^{2}))

\displaystyle+(\nu_{4}-3)\text{tr}(\text{diag}^{2}(\mathbf{G}))\mathbf{a}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\text{diag}(\mathbf{a}_{\ell,i}\mathbf{a}_{\ell,i}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}})\mathbf{a}_{h,i},

(8.44)

where
\nu_{4}:=E(\mathbf{Z}_{i})_{11}^{4}<\infty
by Assumption (R1), and we used Lemma (A.2) of
Li et al., 2019
.
Substitute this back to (
8.43
), we have
E(I_{1})_{\ell,h}^{2}=H_{1}+H_{2}+H_{3}+H_{4}
, where

\displaystyle H_{1}

\displaystyle:=(T^{-2}\text{tr}^{2}(\mathbf{G})-2T^{-1}\text{tr}(\mathbf{G})+1)\bigg(\sum_{j=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}(\mathbf{U}_{\text{-}k,(1)})_{j}^{2}\mathbf{a}_{\ell,j}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{a}_{h,j}\bigg)^{2},

\displaystyle H_{2}

\displaystyle:=T^{-2}\text{tr}(\mathbf{G}^{2})\bigg(\sum_{j=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}(\mathbf{U}_{\text{-}k,(1)})_{j}^{2}\mathbf{a}_{\ell,j}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{a}_{h,j}\bigg)^{2},

\displaystyle H_{3}

\displaystyle:=T^{-2}\text{tr}(\mathbf{G}^{2})\bigg(\sum_{j=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}(\mathbf{U}_{\text{-}k,(1)})_{j}^{2}\mathbf{a}_{\ell,j}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{a}_{\ell,j}\bigg)\bigg(\sum_{j=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}(\mathbf{U}_{\text{-}k,(1)})_{j}^{2}\mathbf{a}_{h,j}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{a}_{h,j}\bigg),

\displaystyle H_{4}

\displaystyle:=T^{-2}\text{tr}(\text{diag}^{2}(\mathbf{G}))\sum_{j=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}(\mathbf{U}_{\text{-}k,(1)})_{j}^{4}(\nu_{4}-3)\mathbf{a}_{h,j}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\text{diag}(\mathbf{a}_{\ell,j}\mathbf{a}_{\ell,j}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}})\mathbf{a}_{h,j}.

By Assumption (RE1), we have

\displaystyle|H_{1}|,|H_{2}|,|H_{3}|

\displaystyle=O(T^{-1})\cdot O(\max_{j\in[d_{\text{-}k}]}\big\|\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\epsilon,j}^{(k)}\big\|_{\max}^{2})=O(T^{-1}),

\displaystyle|H_{4}|

\displaystyle\leq T^{-2}\text{tr}(\mathbf{G}^{2})(\nu_{4}-3)\max_{j\in[d_{\text{-}k}]}\big\|\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\epsilon,j}^{(k)}\big\|_{\max}^{2}=O(T^{-1}).

Hence we can conclude that
\big\|I_{1}\big\|=O_{P}(d_{k}T^{-1/2})
.

For
I_{2}
, define
\widehat{e}_{j}:=(\check{\mathbf{q}}_{\text{-}k}^{(m)}-\mathbf{U}_{\text{-}k,(1)})_{j}
. Then for
\ell,h\in[d_{k}]
, the
(\ell,h)
entry of
I_{2}
is given by

\displaystyle(I_{2})_{\ell,h}

\displaystyle=\sum_{j_{1}=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\widehat{e}_{j_{1}}\bigg(T^{-1}\mathbf{a}_{\ell,j_{1}}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{Z}_{j_{1}}\mathbf{G}\sum_{j_{2}=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}(\mathbf{U}_{\text{-}k,(1)})_{j_{2}}\mathbf{Z}_{j_{2}}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{a}_{h,j_{2}}-(\mathbf{U}_{\text{-}k,(1)})_{j_{1}}\mathbf{a}_{\ell,j_{1}}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{a}_{h,j_{1}}\bigg)=:\sum_{j_{1}=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\widehat{e}_{j_{1}}g_{j_{1},\ell,h}.

By Assumption (RE1) and (E1),

\displaystyle E(g_{j_{1},h,\ell})

\displaystyle=(T^{-1}\text{tr}(\mathbf{G})-1)(\mathbf{U}_{\text{-}k,(1)})_{j_{1}}\mathbf{a}_{\ell,j_{1}}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{a}_{h,j_{1}}=O(T^{-1/2}).

Also, similar to the treatment of
I_{1}
and using (
8.44
),

\displaystyle E(g_{j_{1},h,\ell}^{2})

\displaystyle=T^{-2}\sum_{j=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}(\mathbf{U}_{\text{-}k,(1)})_{j}^{2}E(\mathbf{a}_{\ell,j_{1}}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{Z}_{j_{1}}\mathbf{G}\mathbf{Z}_{j}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{a}_{h,j})^{2}

\displaystyle-2T^{-1}\text{tr}(\mathbf{G})(\mathbf{U}_{\text{-}k,(1)})_{j_{1}}^{2}(\mathbf{a}_{\ell,j_{1}}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{a}_{h,j_{1}})^{2}+(\mathbf{U}_{\text{-}k,(1)})_{j_{1}}^{2}(\mathbf{a}_{\ell,j_{1}}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{a}_{h,j_{1}})^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}(\mathbf{U}_{\text{-}k,(1)})_{j}^{2}T^{-2}\text{tr}(\mathbf{G}^{2})\big\|\mathbf{a}_{h,j}\big\|^{2}\big\|\mathbf{a}_{\ell,j_{1}}\big\|^{2}

\displaystyle+(T^{-1}\text{tr}(\mathbf{G})-1)^{2}(\mathbf{U}_{\text{-}k,(1)})_{j_{1}}^{2}(\mathbf{a}_{\ell,j_{1}}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{a}_{h,j_{1}})^{2}+T^{-2}\text{tr}(\mathbf{G}^{2})(\mathbf{U}_{\text{-}k,(1)})_{j_{1}}^{2}(\mathbf{a}_{\ell,j_{1}}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{a}_{h,j_{1}})^{2}

\displaystyle+T^{-2}(\mathbf{U}_{\text{-}k,(1)})_{j_{1}}^{2}(\nu_{4}-3)\text{tr}(\text{diag}^{2}(\mathbf{G}))\mathbf{a}_{h,j_{1}}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\text{diag}(\mathbf{a}_{\ell,j_{1}}\mathbf{a}_{\ell,j_{1}}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}})\mathbf{a}_{h,j_{1}}=O(T^{-1}),

so that we can conclude that
\big\|I_{2}\big\|=O_{P}\Big(d_{k}\big\|\check{\mathbf{q}}_{\text{-}k}^{(m)}-\mathbf{U}_{\text{-}k,(1)}\big\|\sqrt{\frac{d_{\text{-}k}}{T}}\Big)=O_{P}\Big(d_{k}^{1/2}\big\|\check{\mathbf{q}}_{\text{-}k}^{(m)}-\mathbf{U}_{\text{-}k,(1)}\big\|\sqrt{\frac{d}{T}}\Big)
.

For
I_{3}
, using Theorem 2 of
Latala, 2005
,

\displaystyle\big\|I_{3}\big\|

\displaystyle\leq\big\|\mathbf{Q}_{2,e}^{(k)}\big\|^{2}\big\|T^{-1}\mathbf{Z}_{\epsilon}^{(k)}\mathbf{G}\mathbf{Z}_{\epsilon}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}-\mathbf{I}_{d}\big\|\leq\big\|\check{\mathbf{q}}_{\text{-}k}^{(m)}-\mathbf{U}_{\text{-}k,(1)}\big\|^{2}\max_{j\in[d_{\text{-}k}]}\big\|\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\epsilon,j}^{(k)}\big\|\cdot O_{P}(1+d/T).

Hence

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{S}_{22,m}\big\|

\displaystyle=\big\|I_{0}\big\|+\big\|I_{1}\big\|+2\big\|I_{2}\big\|+\big\|I_{3}\big\|

\displaystyle=O_{P}\bigg(\max_{j\in[d_{\text{-}k}]}\big\|\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\epsilon,j}^{(k)}\big\|+\frac{d_{k}}{\sqrt{T}}+\big\|\check{\mathbf{q}}_{\text{-}k}^{(m)}-\mathbf{U}_{\text{-}k,(1)}\big\|\sqrt{\frac{d_{k}d}{T}}+\max_{j\in[d_{\text{-}k}]}\big\|\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\epsilon,j}^{(k)}\big\|\big\|\check{\mathbf{q}}_{\text{-}k}^{(m)}-\mathbf{U}_{\text{-}k,(1)}\big\|^{2}\Big(\frac{d}{T}+1\Big)\bigg)

\displaystyle=O_{P}\bigg(\max_{j\in[d_{\text{-}k}]}\big\|\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\epsilon,j}^{(k)}\big\|\Big[1+\big\|\check{\mathbf{q}}_{\text{-}k}^{(m)}-\mathbf{U}_{\text{-}k,(1)}\big\|^{2}\frac{d}{T}\Big]+\frac{d_{k}}{\sqrt{T}}+\big\|\check{\mathbf{q}}_{\text{-}k}^{(m)}-\mathbf{U}_{\text{-}k,(1)}\big\|\sqrt{\frac{d_{k}d}{T}}\bigg).

For
\mathbf{S}_{23,m}
, the treatment is exactly parallel to the treatment of
\mathbf{S}_{12,m}
, so that

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{S}_{23,m}\big\|

\displaystyle=\big\|\mathbf{Q}_{3}^{(k)}\big\|\cdot\sqrt{r_{e}d_{k}}\cdot O_{P}(J_{1}+J_{2})

\displaystyle=O_{P}\bigg\{(S_{\psi}^{(k)})^{1/2}\bigg(\sqrt{\frac{r_{e}d_{k}}{T}}+\big\|\check{\mathbf{q}}_{\text{-}k}^{(m)}-\mathbf{U}_{\text{-}k,(1)}\big\|\sqrt{\frac{r_{e}d}{T}}\bigg)\bigg\}.

Finally, by Assumption (RE1) and Theorem 2 of
Latala, 2005
,

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{S}_{33,m}\big\|

\displaystyle\leq\big\|\mathbf{Q}_{3}^{(k)}\big\|^{2}O_{P}\Big(1+\frac{r_{e}}{T}\Big)=O_{P}(S_{\psi}^{(k)}).

This completes the proof of the lemma.
\square

Proof of Theorem
6
.
Firstly, (
8.6
) in Lemma
2

proves that
\mathbf{G}_{k}
has the
j
-th diagonal element with order
d_{k}^{\alpha_{k,j}}
for
j\in[r_{k}]
under Assumption (L1), which is the same as those in
\mathbf{D}_{k}
. Define
\mathbf{B}_{k}\in\mathbb{R}^{d_{k}\otimes d_{k}-1}
to be an orthogonal compliment of
\mathbf{U}_{k,(1)}
, in the sense that
\mathbf{V}_{k}:=(\mathbf{U}_{k,(1)},\mathbf{B}_{k})
has
\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{V}_{k}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}=\mathbf{V}_{k}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{V}_{k}=\mathbf{I}_{d_{k}}
. Then using the SVD of
\mathbf{A}_{k}
in (
3.8
),

\displaystyle\mathbf{V}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{S}_{11,m}^{\prime}\mathbf{V}=\left(\begin{array}[]{cc}\check{g}_{\text{-}k}\mathbf{G}_{k}&\mathbf{0}\\
\mathbf{0}&\mathbf{0}\\
\end{array}\right),

with the
(j,j)
element of
\check{g}_{\text{-}k}\mathbf{G}_{k}
of order
\check{g}_{\text{-}k}d_{k}^{\alpha_{k,j}}
,
j\in[r_{k}]
. By Assumption (L1’), we then have

\text{sep}(\mathbf{U}_{k,(1)}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{S}_{11,m}^{\prime}\mathbf{U}_{k,(1)},\mathbf{B}_{k}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{S}_{11,m}^{\prime}B_{k})\asymp\check{g}_{\text{-}k}d_{k}^{\alpha_{k,1}},

where

\text{sep}(\mathbf{D}_{1},\mathbf{D}_{2}):=\min_{\lambda\in\lambda(\mathbf{D}_{1}),\mu\in\lambda(\mathbf{D}_{2})}|\lambda-\mu|.

Then by Lemma 3 of
Lam et al., 2011
, which is Theorem 8.1.10 in
Golub and Van Loan, 1996
, since
\mathbf{U}_{k,(1)}
is an eigenvector of
\mathbf{S}_{11,m}^{\prime}
and hence the span of
\mathbf{U}_{k,(1)}
is an invariant subspace for
\mathbf{S}_{11,m}^{\prime}
, for the matrix
\mathbf{E}
in (
8.42
), if

\big\|\mathbf{E}\big\|=o_{P}(\check{g}_{\text{-}k}d_{k}^{\alpha_{k,1}}),

then there exists
\check{\mathbf{q}}_{k}^{(m+1)}
which is an eigenvector of
\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}_{y,m+1}^{(k)}
such that

\big\|\check{\mathbf{q}}_{k}^{(m+1)}-\mathbf{U}_{k,(1)}\big\|=O(\big\|\mathbf{E}\big\|/\check{g}_{\text{-}k}d_{k}^{\alpha_{k,1}}).

From the rates proved in Lemma
4
and
5
, with the assumptions
d_{k},r_{e}S_{\psi}^{(k)},\sqrt{T}S_{\psi}^{(k)}=O(\widehat{g}_{\text{-}k}d_{k}^{\alpha_{k,1}})=O(\check{g}_{\text{-}k}d_{k}^{\alpha_{k,1}})
since
\widehat{g}_{\text{-}k}\asymp_{P}\check{g}_{\text{-}k}
by (
4.7
) and (
5.3
), we have

\displaystyle\big\|\check{\mathbf{q}}_{k}^{(m+1)}-\mathbf{U}_{k,(1)}\big\|

\displaystyle=O_{P}(\check{g}_{\text{-}k}^{-1}d_{k}^{-\alpha_{k,1}}\big\|\mathbf{E}\big\|)

\displaystyle=O_{P}\bigg\{\sqrt{\frac{r}{T}}+\check{g}_{\text{-}k}^{-1/2}d_{k}^{-\alpha_{k,1}/2}\big\|\check{\mathbf{q}}_{\text{-}k}^{(m)}-\mathbf{U}_{\text{-}k,(1)}\big\|\sqrt{\frac{rd}{T}}

\displaystyle+\check{g}_{\text{-}k}^{-1}d_{k}^{-\alpha_{k,1}}\max_{j\in[d_{\text{-}k}]}\big\|\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\epsilon,j}^{(k)}\big\|\Big[1+\big\|\check{\mathbf{q}}_{\text{-}k}^{(m)}-\mathbf{U}_{\text{-}k,(1)}\big\|^{2}\frac{d}{T}\Big]\bigg\}.

(8.45)

Furthermore, with the assumption
\max_{j\in[d_{\text{-}k}]}\big\|\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\epsilon,j}^{(k)}\big\|=O\Big(\prod_{j=1}^{K}d_{j}^{\alpha_{j,1}}\sqrt{\frac{r}{T}}\Big)=O_{P}\Big(\check{g}_{\text{-}k}d_{k}^{\alpha_{k,1}}\sqrt{\frac{r}{T}}\Big)
, (
8.45
) becomes

\displaystyle\big\|\check{\mathbf{q}}_{k}^{(m+1)}-\mathbf{U}_{k,(1)}\big\|

\displaystyle=O_{P}\bigg\{\sqrt{\frac{r}{T}}\Big(1+\big\|\check{\mathbf{q}}_{\text{-}k}^{(m)}-\mathbf{U}_{\text{-}k,(1)}\big\|^{2}\frac{d}{T}\Big)+\check{g}_{\text{-}k}^{-1/2}d_{k}^{-\alpha_{k,1}/2}\big\|\check{\mathbf{q}}_{\text{-}k}^{(m)}-\mathbf{U}_{\text{-}k,(1)}\big\|\sqrt{\frac{rd}{T}}\bigg\}.

(8.46)

Putting
m=0
in the above, noting that
\check{\mathbf{q}}_{k}^{(0)}=\widehat{\mathbf{q}}_{k,pre}=\widehat{\mathbf{U}}_{k,pre,(1)}
(see the arguments at the beginning of Section
4.1
) leading to the rate
\big\|\widehat{\mathbf{U}}_{\text{-}k,pre,(1)}-\mathbf{U}_{\text{-}k,(1)}\big\|=O_{P}(b_{k})
in (
4.6
), we also have
\big\|\check{\mathbf{q}}_{\text{-}k}^{(0)}-\mathbf{U}_{\text{-}k,(1)}\big\|=O_{P}(b_{k})
, which upon substituting into (
8.46
), gives the first statement in Theorem
6
.

By defining
e_{m,k}:=\big\|\check{\mathbf{q}}_{k}^{(m)}-\mathbf{U}_{k,(1)}\big\|
and
e_{m,\text{-}k}:=\big\|\check{\mathbf{q}}_{\text{-}k}^{(m)}-\mathbf{U}_{\text{-}k,(1)}\big\|
, we have from an argument similar to the one in (
4.6
) that

e_{m,\text{-}k}=O_{P}\bigg(\sum_{j=1;j\neq k}^{K}e_{m,j}\bigg).

(8.47)

We see from (
8.45
) that the coefficient rates of
\big\|\check{\mathbf{q}}_{\text{-}k}^{(m)}-\mathbf{U}_{\text{-}k,(1)}\big\|
and
\big\|\check{\mathbf{q}}_{\text{-}k}^{(m)}-\mathbf{U}_{\text{-}k,(1)}\big\|^{2}
are respectively, for each
k\in[K]
,

u_{1k}:=\check{g}_{\text{-}k}^{-1/2}d_{k}^{-\alpha_{k,1}/2}\sqrt{\frac{rd}{T}},\;\;\;u_{2k}:=\check{g}_{\text{-}k}^{-1}d_{k}^{-\alpha_{k,1}}\max_{j\in[d_{\text{-}k}]}\big\|\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\epsilon,j}^{(k)}\big\|\frac{d}{T},

which are in fact
o_{P}(K^{-1})
by the further assumption in Theorem
6
.
From (
8.45
), defining
u_{k}:=u_{1k}+u_{2k}
,

\displaystyle e_{m+n,k}

\displaystyle=O_{P}\bigg(\sqrt{\frac{r}{T}}+u_{1k}e_{m+n-1,\text{-}k}+u_{2k}e_{m+n-1,\text{-}k}^{2}\bigg)=O_{P}\bigg(\sqrt{\frac{r}{T}}+(u_{1k}+u_{2k})e_{m+n-1,\text{-}k}\bigg)

\displaystyle=O_{P}\bigg(\sqrt{\frac{r}{T}}+u_{k}\sum_{j=1;j\neq k}^{K}e_{m+n-1,j}\bigg).

Defining
\mathbf{e}_{m}:=(e_{m,1},\ldots,e_{m,K})^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}
, the above becomes

\displaystyle\mathbf{e}_{m+n}

\displaystyle=O_{P}\bigg(\sqrt{\frac{r}{T}}\mathbf{1}_{K}+\mathbf{W}\mathbf{e}_{m+n-1}\bigg),\;\;\;\text{ where }\;\mathbf{W}:=\left(\begin{array}[]{ccccc}0&u_{1}&u_{1}&\cdots&u_{1}\\
u_{2}&0&u_{2}&\cdots&u_{2}\\
\vdots&\vdots&\ddots&\vdots&\vdots\\
u_{K}&u_{K}&u_{K}&\cdots&0\\
\end{array}\right),

with
\big\|\mathbf{W}\big\|_{\infty}\leq K\max_{k\in[K]}u_{k}=o_{P}(1)
by the further assumption in Theorem
6
. Hence iterating the above
n-1
more times, we have

\displaystyle\mathbf{e}_{m+n}

\displaystyle=O_{P}\bigg(\sqrt{\frac{r}{T}}\big(\mathbf{I}_{K}+\mathbf{W}+\cdots+\mathbf{W}^{n-1}\big)\mathbf{1}_{K}+\mathbf{W}^{n}\mathbf{e}_{m}\bigg),\;\text{ implying }

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{e}_{m+n}\big\|_{\max}

\displaystyle=O_{P}\bigg(\sqrt{\frac{r}{T}}\big(1+\big\|\mathbf{W}\big\|_{\infty}+\cdots+\big\|\mathbf{W}\big\|_{\infty}^{n-1}\big)+\big\|\mathbf{W}\big\|_{\infty}^{n}\big\|\mathbf{e}_{m}\big\|_{\max}\bigg)

\displaystyle=O_{P}\bigg(\sqrt{\frac{r}{T}}+\big\|\mathbf{W}\big\|_{\infty}^{n}\big\|\mathbf{e}_{m}\big\|_{\max}\bigg)=O_{P}\bigg(\sqrt{\frac{r}{T}}\bigg)

for
n
large enough. This completes the proof of the theorem.
\square

Proof of Theorem
7
.
Using the notations in (
8.40
) and in Lemma
5
, since
\mathbf{S}_{11,m}
is sandwiched by
\mathbf{A}_{k}
and
\mathbf{A}_{k}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}
, implying that it is sandwiched by
\mathbf{U}_{k}
and
\mathbf{U}_{k}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}
, the span of the columns of
\mathbf{U}_{k}
forms an invariant subspace for
\mathbf{S}_{11,m}
. Now Let
\mathbf{B}_{k}
be the orthogonal complement of
\mathbf{U}_{k}
, in the sense that
\mathbf{U}:=(\mathbf{U}_{k},\mathbf{B}_{k})
is an orthogonal matrix. Then we have

\displaystyle\mathbf{U}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{S}_{11}\mathbf{U}

\displaystyle=\left(\begin{array}[]{cc}\mathbf{U}_{k}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{S}_{11,m}\mathbf{U}_{k}&\mathbf{0}\\
\mathbf{0}&\mathbf{0}\\
\end{array}\right),\;\text{ where }\;\text{sep}(\mathbf{U}_{k}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{S}_{11,m}\mathbf{U}_{k},\mathbf{0})=\lambda_{r_{k}}(\mathbf{U}_{k}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{S}_{11,m}\mathbf{U}_{k}),\;\text{ and }

\displaystyle\lambda_{r_{k}}(\mathbf{U}_{k}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{S}_{11}\mathbf{U}_{k})

\displaystyle=\lambda_{r_{k}}\big(\mathbf{V}_{k}\mathbf{G}_{k}\mathbf{V}_{k}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}(\mathbf{I}_{r_{k}}\otimes\check{\mathbf{q}}_{\text{-}k}^{(m){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k})T^{-1}\mathbf{Z}_{f}^{(k)}\mathcal{A}_{f,T}\mathbf{M}_{T}\mathcal{A}_{f,T}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{Z}_{f}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}(\mathbf{I}_{r_{k}}\otimes\check{\mathbf{q}}_{\text{-}k}^{(m){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k})^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\big)

\displaystyle\geq\lambda_{r_{k}}(\mathbf{G}_{k})\lambda_{r_{k}}(\check{g}_{\text{-}k}\mathbf{I}_{r_{k}})\lambda_{r}(T^{-1}\mathbf{Z}_{f}^{(k)}\mathcal{A}_{f,T}\mathbf{M}_{T}\mathcal{A}_{f,T}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{Z}_{f}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}})\asymp_{P}(\check{g}_{\text{-}k}d_{k}^{\alpha_{k,r_{k}}}),

where we used Theorem 2.8 of
Wang and Paul, 2014
(applicable since
r_{k}=o(T^{1/3})
by Assumption (L1) and all variables are of bounded fourth moments by Assumption (R1)) to conclude that

\displaystyle\lambda_{r}(T^{-1}\mathbf{Z}_{f}^{(k)}\mathcal{A}_{f,T}\mathbf{M}_{T}\mathcal{A}_{f,T}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{Z}_{f}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}})

\displaystyle\asymp_{P}(N+1)\bigg(\frac{\text{tr}(\mathcal{A}_{f,T}\mathbf{M}_{t}\mathcal{A}_{f,T}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}})}{(N+1)T}+\sqrt{\frac{r}{(N+1)T}}\bigg)=1+O(T^{-1/2}),

(8.48)

where the last equality used Assumption (RE1).
Hence Lemma 3 of
Lam et al., 2011
implies that there exists
\check{\mathbf{U}}_{k}
with
\check{\mathbf{U}}_{k}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\check{\mathbf{U}}_{k}=\mathbf{I}_{r_{k}}
and

\big\|\check{\mathbf{U}}_{k}-\mathbf{U}_{k}\big\|=O_{P}\big\{[\big\|\mathbf{S}_{12,m}\big\|+\big\|\mathbf{S}_{13,m}\big\|+\big\|\mathbf{S}_{22,m}\big\|+\big\|\mathbf{S}_{23,m}\big\|+\big\|\mathbf{S}_{33,m}\big\|]/\text{sep}(\mathbf{U}_{k}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{S}_{11,m}\mathbf{U}_{k},\mathbf{0})\big\},

such that
\check{\mathbf{U}}_{k}
equals the
r_{k}
eigenvectors corresponding to the first
r_{k}
largest eigenvalues of
\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}_{y,m+1}^{(k)}
multiplied with an orthogonal matrix, if we can show that the above rate is
o_{P}(1)
. To this end, by Lemma
5
, with the assumption
d_{k},r_{e}S_{\psi}^{(k)}=O(\widehat{g}_{\text{-}k}d_{k}^{\alpha_{k,1}})
, we have
\big\|\mathbf{S}_{23,m}\big\|=O_{P}(\big\|\mathbf{S}_{12,m}\big\|)
, and so with
\widehat{g}_{\text{-}k}\asymp_{P}\check{g}_{\text{-}k}
by (
4.7
) and (
5.3
), we have

\displaystyle\big\|\check{\mathbf{U}}_{k}-\mathbf{U}_{k}\big\|

\displaystyle=O_{P}\bigg\{\check{g}_{\text{-}k}^{-1/2}d_{k}^{\alpha_{k,1}/2-\alpha_{k,r_{k}}}\bigg[\sqrt{rd_{k}}\bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}+\big\|\check{\mathbf{q}}_{\text{-}k}^{(m)}-\mathbf{U}_{\text{-}k,(1)}\big\|\sqrt{\frac{d_{\text{-}k}}{T}}\bigg)+\sqrt{\frac{rr_{e}S_{\psi}^{(k)}}{T}}\bigg]

\displaystyle+\check{g}_{\text{-}k}^{-1}d_{k}^{-\alpha_{k,r_{k}}}\bigg[\max_{j\in[d_{\text{-}k}]}\big\|\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\epsilon,j}^{(k)}\big\|\bigg\{1+\big\|\check{\mathbf{q}}_{\text{-}k}^{(m)}-\mathbf{U}_{\text{-}k,(1)}\big\|^{2}\frac{d}{T}\bigg\}+S_{\psi}^{(k)}\bigg]\bigg\}.

(8.49)

If
m
is large enough, then following Theorem
6
, we have by (
8.47
) that

\big\|\check{\mathbf{q}}_{\text{-}k}^{(m)}-\mathbf{U}_{\text{-}k,(1)}\big\|=O_{P}\bigg(\sum_{j=1;j\neq k}^{K}\big\|\check{\mathbf{q}}_{j}^{(m)}-\mathbf{U}_{j,(1)}\big\|\bigg)=O_{P}\bigg(K\sqrt{\frac{r}{T}}\bigg).

Substituting the above rate into (
8.49
) completes the proof, noting that by (
5.3
) we have
\check{g}_{\text{-}k}d_{k}^{\alpha_{k,1}}\asymp_{P}g_{s}
.
\square

Proof of Theorem
8
.
First, define

\displaystyle\mathbf{Qm}_{1}^{(k)}

\displaystyle:=\text{diag}^{-1/2}(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{y,m+1}^{(k)})\mathbf{A}_{k}(\mathbf{I}_{r_{k}}\otimes\check{\mathbf{q}}_{\text{-}k}^{(m){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k}),

\displaystyle\mathbf{Qm}_{2}^{(k)}

\displaystyle:=\text{diag}^{-1/2}(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{y,m+1}^{(k)})(\check{\mathbf{q}}_{\text{-}k}^{(m){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}\otimes\mathbf{I}_{d_{k}})\text{diag}^{1/2}(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\epsilon,1}^{(k)},\ldots,\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\epsilon,d_{\text{-}k}}^{(k)}),

(8.50)

\displaystyle\mathbf{Qm}_{3}^{(k)}

\displaystyle:=\text{diag}^{-1/2}(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{y,m+1}^{(k)})(\check{\mathbf{q}}_{\text{-}k}^{(m){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}\otimes\mathbf{I}_{d_{k}})\boldsymbol{\Psi}^{(k)},

where
\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{y,m+1}^{(k)}
is defined in (
5.4
). Then we have

\mathbf{R}_{y,m+1}^{(k)}=\sum_{j=1}^{3}\mathbf{Qm}_{j}^{(k)}\mathbf{Qm}_{j}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}.

Consider

\displaystyle\text{tr}(\mathbf{Qm}_{1}^{(k)}\mathbf{Qm}_{1}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}})

\displaystyle=\text{tr}(\text{diag}^{-1/2}(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{y,m+1}^{(k)})\cdot\check{g}_{\text{-}k}\mathbf{A}_{k}\mathbf{A}_{k}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\cdot\text{diag}^{-1/2}(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{y,m+1}^{(k)}))

\displaystyle=\text{tr}(\check{g}_{\text{-}k}^{-1/2}\text{diag}^{-1/2}(\mathbf{A}_{k}\mathbf{A}_{k}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}})(1+o(1))^{-1}\cdot\check{g}_{\text{-}k}\mathbf{A}_{k}\mathbf{A}_{k}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\cdot\check{g}_{\text{-}k}^{-1/2}\text{diag}^{-1/2}(\mathbf{A}_{k}\mathbf{A}_{k}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}})(1+o(1))^{-1})

\displaystyle=d_{k}(1+o(1)),

(8.51)

where the second inequality used (
5.5
