). At the same time, for
j\in[r_{k}]
,

\displaystyle\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{Qm}_{1}^{(k)}\mathbf{Qm}_{1}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}})

\displaystyle=\lambda_{j}(\text{diag}^{-1/2}(\mathbf{A}_{k}\mathbf{A}_{k}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}})(1+o(1))\mathbf{A}_{k}\mathbf{A}_{k}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\text{diag}^{-1/2}(\mathbf{A}_{k}\mathbf{A}_{k}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}})(1+o(1))

\displaystyle=\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{U}_{k}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\text{diag}^{-1}(\mathbf{A}_{k}\mathbf{A}_{k}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}})\mathbf{U}_{k}\mathbf{G}_{k}(1+o(1))),\;\text{ with }

\displaystyle\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{G}_{k})\lambda_{\min}(\text{diag}^{-1}(\mathbf{A}_{k}\mathbf{A}_{k}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}))(1+o(1))

\displaystyle\leq\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{U}_{k}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\text{diag}^{-1}(\mathbf{A}_{k}\mathbf{A}_{k}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}})\mathbf{U}_{k}\mathbf{G}_{k}(1+o(1)))

\displaystyle\leq\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{G}_{k})\lambda_{\max}(\text{diag}^{-1}(\mathbf{A}_{k}\mathbf{A}_{k}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}))(1+o(1)),

so that by assumption (RE2), there are generic constants
c,C>0
such that

d_{k}^{\alpha_{k,j}}\asymp_{P}c\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{G}_{k})\leq\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{Qm}_{1}^{(k)}\mathbf{Qm}_{1}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}})\leq C\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{G}_{k})\asymp_{P}d_{k}^{\alpha_{k,j}}

(8.52)

in probability, where
\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{G}_{k})
being asymptotic in probability to
d_{k}^{\alpha_{k,j}}
is given by (
8.6
) in Lemma
2
.
Using (
8.52
), there exists a constant
C>0
(generic, different from the above) so that
for
j\in[r_{k}]
,

\displaystyle\frac{\lambda_{1}(\mathbf{Qm}_{1}^{(k)}\mathbf{Qm}_{1}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}})}{\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{Qm}_{1}^{(k)}\mathbf{Qm}_{1}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}})}

\displaystyle\leq Cd_{k}^{\alpha_{k,1}-\alpha_{k,j}}.

in probability. Hence using (
8.51
), in probability, we have

\displaystyle d_{k}(1+o(1))\leq\text{tr}(\mathbf{Qm}_{1}^{(k)}\mathbf{Qm}_{1}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}})\leq r_{k}\lambda_{1}(\mathbf{Qm}_{1}^{(k)}\mathbf{Qm}_{1}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}})\leq r_{k}\cdot Cd_{k}^{\alpha_{k,1}-\alpha_{k,j}}\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{Qm}_{1}^{(k)}\mathbf{Qm}_{1}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}),

implying that, in probability, there exists a constant
C>0
such that as
T,d_{k}
are large enough,

\displaystyle\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{R}_{y,m+1}^{(k)})\geq\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{Qm}_{1}^{(k)}\mathbf{Qm}_{1}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}})\geq Cd_{k}^{1-\alpha_{k,1}+\alpha_{k,j}}/r_{k}>1,

(8.53)

since
r_{k}=o(d_{k}^{1-\alpha_{k,1}+\alpha_{k,j}})
by (RE2) for
j\in[r_{k}]
. For
j\in[d_{k}]/[r_{k}]
,

\displaystyle\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{R}_{y,m+1}^{(k)})

\displaystyle\leq\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{Qm}_{1}^{(k)}\mathbf{Qm}_{1}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}})+\lambda_{1}(\mathbf{Qm}_{2}^{(k)}\mathbf{Qm}_{2}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}})+\lambda_{1}(\mathbf{Qm}_{3}^{(k)}\mathbf{Qm}_{3}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}})

\displaystyle\leq 0+\big\|\text{diag}^{-1}(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{y,m+1}^{(k)})\big\|\bigg(\sum_{j=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}(\check{\mathbf{q}}_{\text{-}k}^{(m)})_{j}^{2}\big\|\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\epsilon,j}^{(k)}\big\|+\bigg\|\sum_{j=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}(\check{\mathbf{q}}_{\text{-}k}^{(m)})_{j}\boldsymbol{\Psi}_{j}^{(k)}\bigg\|^{2}\bigg)

\displaystyle\leq\check{g}_{\text{-}k}^{-1}\big\|\text{diag}^{-1}(\mathbf{A}_{k}\mathbf{A}_{k}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}})(1+o(1))\big\|\Big(\max_{j\in[d_{\text{-}k}]}\big\|\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\epsilon,j}^{(k)}\big\|+S_{\psi}^{(k)}\Big)

\displaystyle=O_{P}\bigg(\prod_{j=1;j\neq k}^{K}d_{j}^{-\alpha_{j,1}}\Big(\max_{j\in[d_{\text{-}k}]}\big\|\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\epsilon,j}^{(k)}\big\|+S_{\psi}^{(k)}\Big)\bigg)=o_{P}(1),

where the third inequality is in probability after using (
5.5
), and the last line used (
5.3
) and Assumption (RE2). This completes the proof of the theorem.
\square

Before proving Theorem
9
, define

\displaystyle\mathbf{S}_{y,m+1}^{(k)}:=\text{diag}^{-1/2}(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{y,m+1}^{(k)})\widetilde{\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}_{y,m+1}^{(k)}\text{diag}^{-1/2}(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{y,m+1}^{(k)}).

It is then easy to see that

\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{y,m+1}^{(k)}:=\text{diag}^{-1/2}(\mathbf{S}_{y,m+1}^{(k)})\mathbf{S}_{y,m+1}^{(k)}\text{diag}^{-1/2}(\mathbf{S}_{y,m+1}^{(k)}).

We state and prove the following lemma.

Lemma 6
.

Assume all the assumptions in Theorem
9
hold. Then for
j\in[r_{k}]
and
k\in[K]
,

\displaystyle\bigg|\frac{\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{S}_{y,m+1}^{(k)})}{\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{R}_{y,m+1}^{(k)})}-1\bigg|=O\bigg(r_{k}d_{k}^{\alpha_{k,1}-\alpha_{k,j}-1}\sqrt{\frac{r}{T}}\bigg(d_{k}^{\alpha_{k,1}}+Kd_{k}^{\alpha_{k,1}/2}\sqrt{\frac{rd}{T\check{g}_{\text{-}k}}}+\frac{K^{2}r^{1/2}d}{T^{3/2}\check{g}_{\text{-}k}}\max_{j\in[d_{\text{-}k}]}\big\|\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\epsilon,j}^{(k)}\big\|\bigg)\bigg),

\displaystyle\max_{j\in[d_{k}]}\bigg|\frac{\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{S}_{y,m+1}^{(k)})}{\lambda_{j}(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{y,m+1}^{(k)})}-1\bigg|=O_{P}\bigg(\sqrt{\frac{r}{T}}\bigg(1+K\sqrt{\frac{rd}{T\check{g}_{\text{-}k}}}+\frac{K^{2}r^{1/2}d}{T^{3/2}\check{g}_{\text{-}k}}\max_{j\in[d_{\text{-}k}]}\big\|\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\epsilon,j}^{(k)}\big\|\bigg)\bigg),

\displaystyle\max_{j\in[d_{k}]/[r_{k}]}|\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{S}_{y,m+1}^{(k)})-\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{R}_{y,m+1}^{(k)})|=O_{P}\bigg(\check{g}_{\text{-}k}^{-1}\bigg\{\frac{r_{e}S_{\psi}^{(k)}}{\sqrt{T}}+\frac{K\sqrt{rr_{e}S_{\psi}^{(k)}d}}{T}+\max_{j\in[d_{\text{-}k}]}\big\|\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\epsilon,j}^{(k)}\big\|\frac{K^{2}rd}{T^{2}}\bigg\}\bigg).

Proof of Lemma
6
.
Define, using the definition of
\mathbf{S}_{ij,m}
defined in the proof of Lemma
5
,

\mathbf{S}_{ij,r}:=\text{diag}^{-1/2}(\mathbf{S}_{ij,m})\mathbf{S}_{ij,m}\text{diag}^{-1/2}(\mathbf{S}_{ij,m}),\;\;\;i,j=1,2,3.

Then using (
8.50
), for
j\in[r_{k}]
, we can decompose

\displaystyle\bigg|\frac{\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{S}_{y,m+1}^{(k)})}{\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{R}_{y,m+1}^{(k)})}-1\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{R}_{y,m+1}^{(k)})}\bigg(\sum_{i=1}^{3}\big\|\mathbf{S}_{ii,r}-\mathbf{Qm}_{i}^{(k)}\mathbf{Qm}_{i}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}\big\|+2\sum_{i<j}\big\|\mathbf{S}_{ij,r}\big\|+\sum_{i\neq j}\big\|\check{\mathbf{S}}_{ij}\text{diag}^{-1}(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{y,m+1}^{(k)})\big\|\bigg).

From (
5.5
) and Assumption (RE2), we have that

\lambda_{1}(\text{diag}^{-1}(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{y,m+1}^{(k)})),\;\;\;\lambda_{d_{k}}(\text{diag}^{-1}(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{y,m+1}^{(k)}))\asymp\check{g}_{\text{-}k}^{-1}\asymp_{P}\prod_{j=1;j\neq k}^{K}d_{j}^{-\alpha_{j,1}},

where the last order is from (
5.3
). Hence coupled with the results from Lemma
5
and (
8.53
), and the fact that from an argument similar to (
4.6
) and the result of Theorem
6
that for large enough
m
,

\big\|\check{\mathbf{q}}_{\text{-}k}^{(m)}-\mathbf{U}_{\text{-}k,(1)}\big\|=O_{P}\bigg(K\sqrt{\frac{r}{T}}\bigg),

we have

\displaystyle\frac{\big\|\mathbf{S}_{11,r}-\mathbf{Qm}_{1}^{(k)}\mathbf{Qm}_{1}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}\big\|}{\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{R}_{y,m+1}^{(k)})}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\big\|\text{diag}^{-1}(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{y,m+1}^{(k)})\big\|\big\|\mathbf{S}_{11,m}^{\prime\prime}\big\|}{\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{R}_{y,m+1}^{(k)})}=O_{P}\bigg(r_{k}d_{k}^{\alpha_{k,1}-\alpha_{k,j}-1}\check{g}_{\text{-}k}^{-1}\cdot\check{g}_{\text{-}k}d_{k}^{\alpha_{k,1}}\sqrt{\frac{r}{T}}\bigg)

\displaystyle=O_{P}\bigg(r_{k}d_{k}^{2\alpha_{k,1}-\alpha_{k,j}-1}\sqrt{\frac{r}{T}}\bigg),

\displaystyle\frac{\big\|\mathbf{S}_{22,r}-\mathbf{Qm}_{2}^{(k)}\mathbf{Qm}_{2}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}\big\|}{\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{R}_{y,m+1}^{(k)})}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\big\|\text{diag}^{-1}(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{y,m+1}^{(k)})\big\|\big\|\mathbf{S}_{22,m}-\mathbf{Qm}_{2}^{(k)}\mathbf{Qm}_{2}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}\big\|}{\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{R}_{y,m+1}^{(k)})}

\displaystyle=O_{P}\bigg(r_{k}d_{k}^{\alpha_{k,1}-\alpha_{k,j}-1}\check{g}_{\text{-}k}^{-1}\cdot\bigg\{\max_{j\in[d_{\text{-}k}]}\big\|\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\epsilon,j}^{(k)}\big\|\cdot\frac{K^{2}rd}{T^{2}}+\frac{d_{k}}{\sqrt{T}}+\frac{K\sqrt{rd_{k}d}}{T}\bigg\}\bigg),

\displaystyle\frac{\big\|\mathbf{S}_{33,r}-\mathbf{Qm}_{3}^{(k)}\mathbf{Qm}_{3}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}\big\|}{\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{R}_{y,m+1}^{(k)})}

\displaystyle=O_{P}\bigg(r_{k}d_{k}^{\alpha_{k,1}-\alpha_{k,j}-1}\check{g}_{\text{-}k}^{-1}S_{\psi}^{(k)}\cdot\frac{r_{e}}{\sqrt{T}}\bigg)=O_{P}\bigg(r_{k}d_{k}^{\alpha_{k,1}-\alpha_{k,j}-1}\frac{r_{e}S_{\psi}^{(k)}}{\check{g}_{\text{-}k}\sqrt{T}}\bigg),

\displaystyle\frac{\big\|\mathbf{S}_{12,r}\big\|}{\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{R}_{y,m+1}^{(k)})}

\displaystyle=O_{P}\bigg(r_{k}d_{k}^{3\alpha_{k,1}/2-\alpha_{k,j}-1}\check{g}_{\text{-}k}^{-1/2}\bigg(\sqrt{\frac{rd_{k}}{T}}+\frac{Kr\sqrt{d}}{T}\bigg)\bigg),

\displaystyle\frac{\big\|\mathbf{S}_{13,r}\big\|}{\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{R}_{y,m+1}^{(k)})}

\displaystyle=O_{P}\bigg(r_{k}d_{k}^{3\alpha_{k,1}/2-\alpha_{k,j}-1}\check{g}_{\text{-}k}^{-1/2}\sqrt{\frac{rr_{e}S_{\psi}^{(k)}}{T}}\bigg),

\displaystyle\frac{\big\|\mathbf{S}_{23,r}\big\|}{\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{R}_{y,m+1}^{(k)})}

\displaystyle=O_{P}\bigg(r_{k}d_{k}^{\alpha_{k,1}-\alpha_{k,j}-1}\check{g}_{\text{-}k}^{-1}(S_{\psi}^{(k)})^{1/2}\bigg(\sqrt{\frac{r_{e}d_{k}}{T}}+\frac{K\sqrt{rr_{e}d}}{T}\bigg)\bigg),

\displaystyle\frac{\big\|\check{\mathbf{S}}_{ij}\text{diag}^{-1}(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{y,m+1}^{(k)})\big\|}{\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{R}_{y,m+1}^{(k)})}

\displaystyle=O_{P}\bigg(r_{k}d_{k}^{\alpha_{k,1}-\alpha_{k,j}-1}\check{g}_{\text{-}k}^{-1}T^{-1}\bigg).

These implies that

\displaystyle\bigg|\frac{\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{S}_{y,m+1}^{(k)})}{\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{R}_{y,m+1}^{(k)})}-1\bigg|

\displaystyle=O\bigg(r_{k}d_{k}^{\alpha_{k,1}-\alpha_{k,j}-1}\sqrt{\frac{r}{T}}\bigg(d_{k}^{\alpha_{k,1}}+Kd_{k}^{\alpha_{k,1}/2}\sqrt{\frac{rd}{T\check{g}_{\text{-}k}}}+\frac{K^{2}r^{1/2}d}{T^{3/2}\check{g}_{\text{-}k}}\max_{j\in[d_{\text{-}k}]}\big\|\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\epsilon,j}^{(k)}\big\|\bigg)\bigg),

which is the first statement in the lemma.

For the second statement, for
j\in[d_{\text{-}k}]
and
k\in[K]
, defining
\mathbf{u}_{j}
to be a unit vector with the
j
-th position and 0 elsewhere,

\displaystyle\bigg|\frac{\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{S}_{y,m+1}^{(k)})}{\lambda_{j}(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{y,m+1}^{(k)})}-1\bigg|

\displaystyle=\bigg|\frac{\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{S}_{y,m+1}^{(k)})}{\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{S}_{y,m+1}^{(k)}\text{diag}^{-1}(\mathbf{S}_{y,m+1}^{(k)}))}-1\bigg|\leq\max_{j\in[d_{\text{-}k}]}|\lambda_{j}(\text{diag}(\mathbf{S}_{y,m+1}^{(k)}))-1|

\displaystyle=\max_{j\in[d_{\text{-}k}]}|\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{S}_{y,m+1}^{(k)}-\mathbf{R}_{y,m+1}^{(k)})|=\max_{j\in[d_{\text{-}k}]}|\mathbf{u}_{j}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}(\mathbf{S}_{y,m+1}^{(k)}-\mathbf{R}_{y,m+1}^{(k)})\mathbf{u}_{j}|

\displaystyle\leq\max_{j\in[d_{\text{-}k}]}\bigg|\mathbf{u}_{j}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\bigg(\mathbf{S}_{11,r}^{(k)}-\mathbf{Qm}_{1}^{(k)}\mathbf{Qm}_{1}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}\bigg)\mathbf{u}_{j}\bigg|+2\max_{j\in[d_{\text{-}k}]}|\mathbf{u}_{j}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{S}_{12,r}^{(k)}\mathbf{u}_{j}|+2\max_{j\in[d_{\text{-}k}]}|\mathbf{u}_{j}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{S}_{13,r}^{(k)}\mathbf{u}_{j}|

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=2}^{3}|\mathbf{u}_{j}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}(\mathbf{S}_{ii,r}^{(k)}-\mathbf{Qm}_{i}^{(k)}\mathbf{Qm}_{i}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}})\mathbf{u}_{j}|+2|\mathbf{u}_{j}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{S}_{23,r}^{(k)}\mathbf{u}_{j}|+\sum_{i\neq j}\big\|\text{diag}^{-1}(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{y,m+1}^{(k)})\check{\mathbf{S}}_{ij}\big\|.

But similar to the above,

\displaystyle\max_{j\in[d_{\text{-}k}]}|\mathbf{u}_{j}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}(\mathbf{S}_{11,r}-\mathbf{Qm}_{1}^{(k)}\mathbf{Qm}_{1}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}})\mathbf{u}_{j}|=O_{P}(\big\|T^{-1}\mathbf{Z}_{f}^{(k)}\mathcal{A}_{f,T}\mathbf{M}_{T}\mathcal{A}_{f,T}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{Z}_{f}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}-\mathbf{I}_{r}\big\|)=O_{P}\bigg(\sqrt{\frac{r}{T}}\bigg),

\displaystyle\max_{j\in[d_{\text{-}k}]}|\mathbf{u}_{j}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{S}_{12,r}^{(k)}\mathbf{u}_{j}|=O_{P}\bigg(\check{g}_{\text{-}k}^{-1/2}\bigg(\sqrt{\frac{rd_{k}}{T}}+\frac{Kr\sqrt{d}}{T}\bigg)\bigg),\;\;\;\max_{j\in[d_{\text{-}k}]}|\mathbf{u}_{j}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{S}_{13,r}\mathbf{u}_{j}|=O_{P}\bigg(\check{g}_{\text{-}k}^{-1/2}\sqrt{\frac{rr_{e}S_{\psi}^{(k)}}{T}}\bigg),

\displaystyle\max_{j\in[d_{\text{-}k}]}|\mathbf{u}_{j}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}(\mathbf{S}_{22,r}-\mathbf{Qm}_{2}^{(k)}\mathbf{Qm}_{2}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}})\mathbf{u}_{j}|=O_{P}\bigg(\check{g}_{\text{-}k}^{-1}\bigg\{\frac{K^{2}rd}{T^{2}}\max_{j\in[d_{\text{-}k}]}\big\|\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\epsilon,j}^{(k)}\big\|+\frac{d_{k}}{\sqrt{T}}+\frac{K\sqrt{rd_{k}d}}{T}\bigg\}\bigg),

\displaystyle\max_{j\in[d_{\text{-}k}]}|\mathbf{u}_{j}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{S}_{23,r}^{(k)}\mathbf{u}_{j}|=O_{P}\bigg(\check{g}_{\text{-}k}^{-1}(S_{\psi}^{(k)})^{1/2}\bigg(\sqrt{\frac{r_{e}d_{k}}{T}}+\frac{K\sqrt{rr_{e}d}}{T}\bigg)\bigg),

\displaystyle\max_{j\in[d_{\text{-}k}]}|\mathbf{u}_{j}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}(\mathbf{S}_{33,r}-\mathbf{Qm}_{3}^{(k)}\mathbf{Qm}_{3}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}})\mathbf{u}_{j}|=O_{P}\bigg(\frac{r_{e}S_{\psi}^{(k)}}{\check{g}_{\text{-}k}\sqrt{T}}\bigg),\;\;\;\big\|\text{diag}^{-1}(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{y,m+1}^{(k)})\check{\mathbf{S}}_{ij}\big\|=O_{P}(\check{g}_{\text{-}k}^{-1}T^{-1}).

Hence the above implies that

\displaystyle\bigg|\frac{\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{S}_{y,m+1}^{(k)})}{\lambda_{j}(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{y,m+1}^{(k)})}-1\bigg|

\displaystyle=O_{P}\bigg(\sqrt{\frac{r}{T}}\bigg(1+K\sqrt{\frac{rd}{T\check{g}_{\text{-}k}}}+\frac{K^{2}r^{1/2}d}{T^{3/2}\check{g}_{\text{-}k}}\max_{j\in[d_{\text{-}k}]}\big\|\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\epsilon,j}^{(k)}\big\|\bigg)\bigg),

which is the second statement of the lemma.
Finally, consider for
j\in[d_{k}]/[r_{k}]
,

\displaystyle|\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{S}_{y,m+1}^{(k)})-\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{R}_{y,m+1}^{(k)})|

\displaystyle\leq\bigg|\lambda_{j}\bigg(\mathbf{S}_{11,r}+\sum_{j=2}^{3}(\mathbf{S}_{1j,r}+\mathbf{S}_{j1,r}+\mathbf{Qm}_{j}^{(k)}\mathbf{Qm}_{j}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}})\bigg)-\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{R}_{y,m+1}^{(k)})\bigg|

\displaystyle+2\big\|\mathbf{S}_{23,r}\big\|+\sum_{j=2}^{3}\big\|\mathbf{S}_{jj,r}-\mathbf{Qm}_{j}^{(k)}\mathbf{Qm}_{j}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}\big\|+\sum_{i\neq j}\big\|\text{diag}^{-1}(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{y,m+1}^{(k)})\check{\mathbf{S}}_{ij}^{(k)}\big\|

\displaystyle=2\big\|\mathbf{S}_{23,r}\big\|+\sum_{j=2}^{3}\big\|\mathbf{S}_{jj,r}-\mathbf{Qm}_{j}^{(k)}\mathbf{Qm}_{j}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}\big\|+\sum_{i\neq j}\big\|\text{diag}^{-1}(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{y,m+1}^{(k)})\check{\mathbf{S}}_{ij}^{(k)}\big\|,

(8.54)

if we can show that for large enough
T,d_{k}
, in probability, for
j\in[d_{k}]/[r_{k}]
,

\lambda_{j}\bigg(\mathbf{S}_{11,r}+\sum_{j=2}^{3}(\mathbf{S}_{1j,r}+\mathbf{S}_{j1,r}+\mathbf{Qm}_{j}^{(k)}\mathbf{Qm}_{j}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}})\bigg)=\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{R}_{y,m+1}^{(k)}).

(8.55)

With (
8.54
), from the rates obtained in previous arguments, we then have

\displaystyle|\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{S}_{y,m+1}^{(k)})-\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{R}_{y,m+1}^{(k)})|=O_{P}\bigg(\check{g}_{\text{-}k}^{-1}\bigg\{\frac{r_{e}S_{\psi}^{(k)}}{\sqrt{T}}+\frac{K\sqrt{rr_{e}S_{\psi}^{(k)}d}}{T}+\max_{j\in[d_{\text{-}k}]}\big\|\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\epsilon,j}^{(k)}\big\|\frac{K^{2}rd}{T^{2}}\bigg\}\bigg),

which is the third statement of the lemma. Hence it remains to show (
8.55
). To this end,
define the kernel of
\mathbf{A}_{k}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\text{diag}^{-1/2}(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{y,m+1}^{(k)})
to be

\displaystyle\mathcal{N}:=\{\mathbf{w}:\mathbf{A}_{k}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\text{diag}^{-1/2}(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{y,m+1}^{(k)})\mathbf{w}=\mathbf{0}\}\equiv\{\mathbf{w}:\mathbf{A}_{k}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{w}=\mathbf{0}\}

in probability by (
5.5
) and assumption (RE2), and define
\mathcal{N}^{\perp}
the corresponding row space, which has
\mathcal{N}^{\perp}=\text{span}(\mathbf{A}_{k})
in probability by the above argument. Hence by Assumption (L1) that
\mathbf{A}_{k}
is of full rank, dimension of
\mathcal{N}^{\perp}
is exactly
r_{k}
(in probability), and the rank-nullity theorem implies that, in probability, the dimension of
\mathcal{N}
is exactly
d_{k}-r_{k}
. Consider, by (
8.48
) and Assumption (L1), for some constant
c>0
, we have in probability that

\displaystyle\lambda_{r_{k}}(\mathbf{S}_{11,r})

\displaystyle\geq\check{g}_{\text{-}k}\min_{\mathbf{w}\in\mathcal{N}^{\perp}}\big\|\mathbf{w}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\text{diag}^{-1/2}(\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{y,m+1}^{(k)})\mathbf{A}_{k}\big\|^{2}\lambda_{r}(T^{-1}\mathbf{Z}_{f}^{(k)}\mathcal{A}_{f,T}\mathbf{M}_{T}\mathcal{A}_{f,T}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{Z}_{f}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}})\geq cd_{k}^{\alpha_{k,r_{k}}}.

At the same time, we have

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{Qm}_{2}^{(k)}\mathbf{Qm}_{2}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}+\mathbf{Qm}_{3}^{(k)}\mathbf{Qm}_{3}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}\big\|

\displaystyle=O_{P}(\check{g}_{\text{-}k}^{-1}(\max_{j\in[d_{\text{-}k}]}\big\|\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\epsilon,j}^{(k)}\big\|+S_{\psi}^{(k)})),

and earlier calculations shown that

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{S}_{12,r}\big\|

\displaystyle=O_{P}\bigg(\check{g}_{\text{-}k}^{-1/2}d_{k}^{\alpha_{k,1}/2}\bigg(\sqrt{\frac{rd_{k}}{T}}+\frac{Kr\sqrt{d}}{T}\bigg)\bigg),\;\;\;\big\|\mathbf{S}_{13,r}\big\|=O_{P}\bigg(\check{g}_{\text{-}k}^{-1/2}d_{k}^{\alpha_{k,1}/2}\sqrt{\frac{rr_{e}S_{\psi}^{(k)}}{T}}\bigg).

Hence in probability, the eigenvalues of
\mathbf{S}_{11,r}
are dominating those in

\mathbf{G}:=\sum_{j=2}^{3}(\mathbf{S}_{1j,r}+\mathbf{S}_{j1,r}+\mathbf{Qm}_{j}^{(k)}\mathbf{Qm}_{j}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}).

Hence the
r_{k}
eigenvectors corresponding to the largest
r_{k}
eigenvalues of
\mathbf{G}+\mathbf{S}_{11,r}^{(k)}
coincide with those of
\mathbf{S}_{11,r}
’s for large enough
d_{k}
, which are all necessarily in
\mathcal{N}^{\perp}
. This means that the
(r_{k}+1)
-th largest eigenvalue of
\mathbf{G}+\mathbf{S}_{11,r}
and beyond will have eigenvectors in
\mathcal{N}
since the dimension of
\mathcal{N}^{\perp}
is
r_{k}
. Take
\mathbf{w}\in\mathcal{N}
, then it is easy to see that

\mathbf{w}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}(\mathbf{G}+\mathbf{S}_{11,r})\mathbf{w}=\mathbf{w}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}(\mathbf{Qm}_{2}^{(k)}\mathbf{Qm}_{2}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}}+\mathbf{Qm}_{3}^{(k)}\mathbf{Qm}_{3}^{(k){\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}})\mathbf{w}=\mathbf{w}^{\mathrm{\scriptscriptstyle T}}\mathbf{R}_{y,m+1}^{(k)}\mathbf{w},

showing that
\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{G}+\mathbf{S}_{11,r})=\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{R}_{y,m+1}^{(k)})
for
j\in[d_{k}]/[r_{k}]
, which is (
8.55
). This completes the proof of the lemma.
\square

Proof of Theorem
9
. For
j\in[r_{k}]
and
k\in[K]
, we can decompose

\displaystyle\lambda_{j}(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{y,m+1}^{(k)})

\displaystyle\leq\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{R}_{y,m+1}^{(k)})\bigg\{1+\bigg|\frac{\lambda_{j}(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{y,m+1}^{(k)})}{\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{S}_{y,m+1}^{(k)})}-1\bigg|+\bigg|\frac{\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{S}_{y,m+1}^{(k)})}{\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{R}_{y,m+1}^{(k)})}-1\bigg|

\displaystyle+\bigg|\frac{\lambda_{j}(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{y,m+1}^{(k)})}{\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{S}_{y,m+1}^{(k)})}-1\bigg|\cdot\bigg|\frac{\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{S}_{y,m+1}^{(k)})}{\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{R}_{y,m+1}^{(k)})}-1\bigg|\bigg\}

\displaystyle=\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{R}_{y,m+1}^{(k)})(1+O_{P}(r_{k}d_{k}^{\alpha_{k,1}-\alpha_{k,j}-1}a_{T}(\delta_{k,1})+a_{T}(0)))

\displaystyle\succeq_{P}r_{k}d_{k}^{1-\alpha_{k,1}+\alpha_{k,j}}(1+O_{P}(r_{k}d_{k}^{\alpha_{k,1}-\alpha_{k,j}-1}a_{T}(\delta_{k,1})+a_{T}(0))),

where the second last line used the results in Lemma
6
, and the last line used the result from Theorem
8
. Similarly, for
j\in[d_{k}]/[r_{k}]
,

\displaystyle\lambda_{j}(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{y,m+1}^{(k)})

\displaystyle\leq\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{R}_{y,m+1}^{(k)})\bigg(1+\bigg|\frac{\lambda_{j}(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{y,m+1}^{(k)})}{\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{S}_{y,m+1}^{(k)})}-1\bigg|\bigg)+|\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{S}_{y,m+1}^{(k)})-\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{R}_{y,m+1}^{(k)})|

\displaystyle+|\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{S}_{y,m+1}^{(k)})-\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{R}_{y,m+1}^{(k)})|\cdot\bigg|\frac{\lambda_{j}(\widehat{\mathbf{R}}_{y,m+1}^{(k)})}{\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{S}_{y,m+1}^{(k)})}-1\bigg|

\displaystyle=\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{R}_{y,m+1}^{(k)})+O_{P}(a_{T}(0))\leq 1+O_{P}(a_{T}(0)),

where the second last line used Assumption (RE2) to conclude that

\max_{j\in[d_{k}]/[r_{k}]}|\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{S}_{y,m+1}^{(k)})-\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{R}_{y,m+1}^{(k)})|=O_{P}(a_{T}(0)),

and the last inequality used a result in Theorem
8
. This completes the proof of the theorem.
\square
